    8:   onerror: ((event: ErrorEvent) => void) | null = null;

   12:     this.commands.push(command);
   13:     if (command === "uci") {
   14:       setTimeout(() => this.send("uciok"), 10);
   15:     } else if (command === "isready") {
   16:       setTimeout(() => this.send("readyok"), 10);
   17:     }
   18:   }
   19:   terminate() {
   20:     this.terminated = true;
   21:   }
   22:   send(data: string) {
   23:     this.onmessage?.({ data } as MessageEvent<string>);
   24:   }
   25: }

   30: function formatUciMove(fen: string, uci: string): string {
   31:   const match = /^([a-h][1-8])([a-h][1-8])([qrbn])?$/.exec(uci);
   32:   if (!match) return uci;
   33:   try {
   34:     const position = new Chess(fen);
   35:     const move = position.move({
   36:       from: match[1] as Square,
   37:       to: match[2] as Square,
   38:       ...(match[3] ? { promotion: match[3] as "q" | "r" | "b" | "n" } : {}),
   39:     });
   40:     return move.san;
   41:   } catch {
   42:     return uci;
   43:   }
   44: }
   45: 
   46: function formatUciPv(fen: string, pv: string[]): string {
   47:   const position = new Chess(fen);
   48:   const san: string[] = [];
   49:   for (const uci of pv) {
   50:     const match = /^([a-h][1-8])([a-h][1-8])([qrbn])?$/.exec(uci);
   51:     if (!match) break;
   52:     try {
   53:       const move = position.move({
   54:         from: match[1] as Square,
   55:         to: match[2] as Square,
   56:         ...(match[3] ? { promotion: match[3] as "q" | "r" | "b" | "n" } : {}),
   57:       });
   58:       san.push(move.san);
   59:     } catch {
   60:       break;
   61:     }
   62:   }
   63:   return san.join(" ");
   64: }

   88: function readableDate(timestamp: number): string {
   89:   if (!timestamp) return "Not opened";
   90:   const elapsed = Date.now() - timestamp;
   91:   if (elapsed < 60_000) return "Just now";
   92:   if (elapsed < 3_600_000) return `${Math.floor(elapsed / 60_000)}m ago`;
   93:   if (elapsed < 86_400_000) return `${Math.floor(elapsed / 3_600_000)}h ago`;
   94:   return new Date(timestamp).toLocaleDateString();
   95: }

  101: function normalizedCandidate(value: CandidateInput, index: number): Candidate {
  102:   const { relative_score, confidence, ...candidate } = value;
  103:   return {
  104:     ...candidate,
  105:     id: candidate.id || `candidate-${index + 1}`,
  106:     source: candidate.source ?? "automatic",
  107:     relativeScore: candidate.relativeScore ?? relative_score ?? confidence ?? 0,
  108:   };
  109: }

  320:       canvas.width = Math.floor(viewport.width);

  339:   useEffect(() => {
  340:     function onKey(event: KeyboardEvent) {
  341:       if (!session || !book || isTypingTarget(event.target)) return;
  342:       if (cropEditingId && ["ArrowLeft", "ArrowRight", "ArrowUp", "ArrowDown"].includes(event.key)) {
  343:         const candidate = (session.candidatesByPage[String(session.page)] ?? []).find((item) => item.id === cropEditingId);
  344:         if (!candidate) return;
  345:         const step = event.shiftKey ? 0.01 : 0.002;
  346:         const x = event.key === "ArrowLeft" ? -step : event.key === "ArrowRight" ? step : 0;
  347:         const y = event.key === "ArrowUp" ? -step : event.key === "ArrowDown" ? step : 0;
  348:         const next = editCrop(candidate, "move", x, y);
  349:         patchSession((current) => {
  350:           const pageKey = String(current.page);
  351:           return {
  352:             ...current,
  353:             candidatesByPage: {
  354:               ...current.candidatesByPage,
  355:               [pageKey]: (current.candidatesByPage[pageKey] ?? []).map((item) =>
  356:                 item.id === candidate.id ? { ...item, ...next } : item),
  357:             },
  358:           };
  359:         });
  360:         event.preventDefault();
  361:         return;
  362:       }
  363:       if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") changePage(-1);
  364:       if (event.key === "ArrowRight") changePage(1);
  365:     }
  366:     window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  367:     return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  368:   });
  369: 
  370:   useEffect(() => () => {
  371:     engineRef.current?.dispose();
  372:     engineRef.current = null;
  373:   }, []);
  374: 
  375:   const sortedBooks = useMemo(() => [...books].sort((a, b) => b.lastOpenedAt - a.lastOpenedAt), [books]);
  376:   const visibleBooks = useMemo(() => {
  377:     const needle = bookSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
  378:     return sortedBooks.filter((item) => {
  379:       const matchesTab = true;
  380:       return matchesTab && item.title.toLowerCase().includes(needle);
  381:     });
  382:   }, [bookSearch, libraryTab, sessionIndex, sortedBooks]);
  383: 
  384:   const openingLine = useMemo(
  385:     () => parseOpeningLine(openingExplorerInput),
  386:     [openingExplorerInput],
  387:   );
  388:   const openingPosition = useMemo(
  389:     () => openingPositionAt(openingExplorerInput, openingExplorerPly),
  390:     [openingExplorerInput, openingExplorerPly],
  391:   );
  392: 
  393:   useEffect(() => {
  394:     if (workspaceMode !== "opening-explorer") return;
  395:     if (!lichessStatus?.connected) {
  396:       setOpeningStats(null);
  397:       setOpeningStatsStatus("idle");
  398:       return;
  399:     }
  400: 
  401:     if (openingPosition.error) {
  402:       setOpeningStats(null);
  403:       setOpeningStatsStatus("idle");
  404:       return;
  405:     }
  406: 
  407:     const controller = new AbortController();
  408:     const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
  409:       setOpeningStatsStatus("loading");
  410:       fetchOpeningExplorerStats(openingPosition.uciMoves, controller.signal)
  411:         .then((result) => {
  412:           setOpeningStats(result);
  413:           setOpeningStatsStatus("ready");
  414:         })
  415:         .catch(() => {
  416:           if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
  417:             setOpeningStats(null);
  418:             setOpeningStatsStatus("error");
  419:           }
  420:         });
  421:     }, 500);
  422: 
  423:     return () => {
  424:       window.clearTimeout(timer);
  425:       controller.abort();
  426:     };
  427:   }, [workspaceMode, openingPosition, openingExplorerPly, lichessStatus?.connected]);
  428: 
  429:   const boardSession: StudyBoardState | null =
  430:     workspaceMode === "analysis"
  431:       ? analysisBoard
  432:       : workspaceMode === "opening-prep"
  433:         ? openingPrepBoard
  434:         : workspaceMode === "opening-explorer"
  435:           ? {
  436:               lineBaseFen: START_FEN,
  437:               currentFen: openingPosition.fen,
  438:               moves: openingPosition.sanMoves,
  439:               moveIndex: openingPosition.sanMoves.length,
  440:               flipped: false,
  441:               coordinates: true,
  442:             }
  443:           : studyMode
  444:             ? studyBoard
  445:             : session;
  446:   const game = useMemo(() => {
  447:     if (!boardSession) return new Chess();
  448:     try {
  449:       return makePosition(boardSession.lineBaseFen, boardSession.moves, boardSession.moveIndex);
  450:     } catch {
  451:       try { return new Chess(boardSession.currentFen); } catch { return new Chess(); }
  452:     }
  453:   }, [boardSession]);
  454:   const legalTargets = useMemo(() => {
  455:     if (!selectedSquare) return new Set<string>();
  456:     return new Set(game.moves({ square: selectedSquare, verbose: true }).map((move) => move.to));
  457:   }, [game, selectedSquare]);
  458:   const pieceSquares = game.board();
  459:   const moveHistory = useMemo(() => {
  460:     try { return makePosition(boardSession?.lineBaseFen ?? START_FEN, boardSession?.moves ?? [], boardSession?.moveIndex ?? 0).history({ verbose: true }); }
  461:     catch { return []; }
  462:   }, [boardSession]);
  463:   const highlightedMove = lastMove ?? (moveHistory.length
  464:     ? { from: moveHistory[moveHistory.length - 1].from, to: moveHistory[moveHistory.length - 1].to }
  465:     : null);
  466:   const engineArrow = useMemo(
  467:     () => engineEnabled ? parseBestMove(engineScore?.bestMove) : null,
  468:     [engineEnabled, engineScore?.bestMove],
  469:   );
  470: 
  471:   useEffect(() => {
  472:     setUserArrows([]);
  473:     setArrowStart(null);
  474:     setArrowHover(null);
  475:     setAnnotationMode(false);
  476:   }, [book?.id, session?.currentFen]);
  477: 
  478:   useEffect(() => {
  479:     if (engineEnabled && boardSession) {
  480:       engineRef.current?.analyze(game.fen(), engineDepth);
  481:     }
  482:   }, [engineEnabled, boardSession?.currentFen, boardSession?.moveIndex, game, engineDepth]);
  483: 
  484:   function messageOf(cause: unknown): string {
  485:     return cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause);
  486:   }
  487: 
  488:   function toggleEngine() {
  489:     if (engineEnabled) {
  490:       engineRef.current?.dispose();
  491:       engineRef.current = null;
  492:       setEngineEnabled(false);
  493:       setEngineStatus("off");
  494:       setEngineScore(null);
  495:       setEngineMessage("");
  496:       return;
  497:     }
  498:     try {
  499:       setEngineScore(null);
  500:       setEngineMessage("");
  501:       const client = new StockfishEngine(
  502:         `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}stockfish/stockfish-19-lite-single.js`,
  503:         (status, message) => {
  504:           setEngineStatus(status);
  505:           if (status === "error") {
  506:             setEngineMessage(message || "Stockfish could not start. Check the engine assets and browser worker support.");
  507:             setEngineEnabled(false);
  508:             setEngineScore(null);
  509:             engineRef.current?.dispose();
  510:             engineRef.current = null;
  511:           }
  512:         },
  513:         setEngineScore,
  514:       );
  515:       engineRef.current = client;
  516:       setEngineEnabled(true);
  517:     } catch (cause) {
  518:       setEngineStatus("error");
  519:       setEngineMessage(`Stockfish could not start: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  520:       setEngineEnabled(false);
  521:     }
  522:   }
  523: 
  524:   function patchSession(change: Partial<BookSession> | ((current: BookSession) => BookSession)) {
  525:     setSession((current) => {
  526:       if (!current) return current;
  527:       return typeof change === "function" ? change(current) : { ...current, ...change };
  528:     });
  529:   }
  530: 
  531:   function patchBoardSession(change: Partial<StudyBoardState>) {
  532:     if (workspaceMode === "opening-explorer") {
  533:       return;
  534:     }
  535: 
  536:     if (workspaceMode === "analysis") {
  537:       setAnalysisBoard((current) => ({ ...current, ...change }));
  538:     } else if (workspaceMode === "opening-prep") {
  539:       setOpeningPrepBoard((current) => ({ ...current, ...change }));
  540:     } else if (studyMode) {
  541:       setStudyBoard((current) => current ? { ...current, ...change } : current);
  542:     } else {
  543:       patchSession(change);
  544:     }
  545:   }
  546:   async function toggleStudyMode() {
  547:     if (!studyMode) {
  548:       if (!session) {
  549:         setError("Open a book before entering study mode.");
  550:         return;
  551:       }
  552:       setStudyBoard({
  553:         lineBaseFen: session.lineBaseFen,
  554:         currentFen: session.currentFen,
  555:         moves: [...session.moves],
  556:         moveIndex: session.moveIndex,
  557:         flipped: session.flipped,
  558:         coordinates: session.coordinates,
  559:       });
  560:       setStudyMode(true);
  561:     } else {
  562:       // Preserve the latest Focus Mode board when returning to normal mode.
  563:       if (studyBoard) {
  564:         patchSession({
  565:           lineBaseFen: studyBoard.lineBaseFen,
  566:           currentFen: studyBoard.currentFen,
  567:           moves: [...studyBoard.moves],
  568:           moveIndex: studyBoard.moveIndex,
  569:           flipped: studyBoard.flipped,
  570:           coordinates: studyBoard.coordinates,
  571:         });
  572:       }
  573:       setStudyMode(false);
  574:     }
  575:     setLastMove(null);
  576:     setSelectedSquare(null);
  577:     setPromotionRequest(null);
  578: 
  579:     try {
  580:       if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
  581:         await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen();
  582:       } else {
  583:         await document.exitFullscreen();
  584:       }
  585:     } catch {
  586:       // Focus layout still works when browser fullscreen is blocked.
  587:     }
  588: }
  589: 
  590:   async function openBook(item: BookRecord) {
  591:     try {
  592:       const existing = sessionIndex[item.id] ?? await getSession(item.id);
  593:       const nextSession = existing ?? newSession(item.id);
  594:       const opened = { ...item, lastOpenedAt: Date.now() };
  595:       await Promise.all([putBook(opened), putSession(nextSession), setActiveBookId(opened.id)]);
  596:       setBooks((current) => [...current.filter((candidate) => candidate.id !== opened.id), opened].sort((a, b) => b.lastOpenedAt - a.lastOpenedAt));
  597:       setSessionIndex((current) => ({ ...current, [opened.id]: nextSession }));
  598:       setBook(opened);
  599:       setSession(nextSession);
  600:       setNotice(`Opened ${opened.title}`);
  601:       setError("");
  602:       setSelectedSquare(null);
  603:     } catch (cause) {
  604:       setError(`Could not open book: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  605:     }
  606:   }
  607: 
  608:   async function addBook(file?: File) {
  609:     if (!file) return;
  610:     try {
  611:       if (file.size > FILE_LIMIT) throw new Error("PDFs must be 100 MB or smaller.");
  612:       const header = new Uint8Array(await file.slice(0, 5).arrayBuffer());
  613:       if (new TextDecoder().decode(header) !== "%PDF-") throw new Error("The selected file does not contain a valid PDF header.");
  614:       const now = Date.now();
  615:       const created: BookRecord = {
  616:         id: crypto.randomUUID(),
  617:         title: file.name.replace(/\.pdf$/i, ""),
  618:         file: file.slice(0, file.size, "application/pdf"),
  619:         byteLength: file.size,
  620:         lastOpenedAt: now,
  621:         addedAt: now,
  622:       };
  623:       const blank = newSession(created.id);
  624:       await Promise.all([putBook(created), putSession(blank), setActiveBookId(created.id)]);
  625:       setBooks((current) => [created, ...current]);
  626:       setSessionIndex((current) => ({ ...current, [created.id]: blank }));
  627:       setBook(created);
  628:       setSession(blank);
  629:       setNotice("PDF added to this browser's local library.");
  630:       setError("");
  631:       if (fileInput.current) fileInput.current.value = "";
  632:     } catch (cause) {
  633:       setError(`Could not add PDF: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  634:     }
  635:   }
  636: 
  637:   async function deleteBook(item: BookRecord) {
  638:     if (!window.confirm(`Delete "${item.title}" and its saved reading progress from this browser? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
  639: 
  640:     try {
  641:       await removeBook(item.id);
  642:       await removeSession(item.id);
  643: 
  644:       setBooks((current) => current.filter((entry) => entry.id !== item.id));
  645:       setSessionIndex((current) => {
  646:         const next = { ...current };
  647:         delete next[item.id];
  648:         return next;
  649:       });
  650: 
  651:       if (book?.id === item.id) {
  652:         setBook(null);
  653:         setSession(null);
  654:         setPdf(null);
  655:       }
  656: 
  657:       setNotice(`Removed "${item.title}" from your library.`);
  658:       setError("");
  659:     } catch (cause) {
  660:       setError(`Could not remove book: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  661:     }
  662:   }
  663:   async function renameBook(item: BookRecord) {
  664:     const title = window.prompt("Book title", item.title)?.trim();
  665:     if (!title || title === item.title) return;
  666:     const renamed = { ...item, title };
  667:     try {
  668:       await putBook(renamed);
  669:       setBooks((current) => current.map((entry) => entry.id === item.id ? renamed : entry));
  670:       if (book?.id === item.id) setBook(renamed);
  671:     } catch (cause) {
  672:       setError(`Could not rename book: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  673:     }
  674:   }
  675: 
  676:   function changePage(delta: number) {
  677:     if (!session) return;
  678:     patchSession({ page: Math.max(1, Math.min(session.pageCount || 999_999, session.page + delta)) });
  679:   }
  680: 
  681:   function goToPage(value: number) {
  682:     if (!session || !Number.isFinite(value)) return;
  683:     patchSession({ page: Math.max(1, Math.min(session.pageCount || 1, Math.floor(value))) });
  684:   }
  685: 
  686:   function moveToPosition(fen: string): boolean {
  687:     const activeSession = session ?? newSession("analysis");
  688:     try {
  689:       const next = new Chess(fen);
  690:       setSession({
  691:         ...activeSession,
  692:         originalFen: fen,
  693:         lineBaseFen: fen,
  694:         currentFen: next.fen(),
  695:         moves: [],
  696:         moveIndex: 0,
  697:       });
  698:       setLastMove(null);
  699:       setSelectedSquare(null);
  700:       setUserArrows([]);
  701:       setNotice("Position loaded. Reset Board returns to this diagram position.");
  702:       return true;
  703:     } catch (cause) {
  704:       setError(`Invalid chess position: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  705:       return false;
  706:     }
  707:   }
  708: 
  709:   function analyzeManualPosition(fen: string): boolean {
  710:     const loaded = moveToPosition(fen);
  711:     if (!loaded) return false;
  712:     setEngineScore(null);
  713:     setEngineMessage("");
  714:     if (!engineEnabled) {
  715:       toggleEngine();
  716:     } else {
  717:       engineRef.current?.analyze(fen, engineDepth);
  718:     }
  719:     setNotice("Manual position loaded. Stockfish analysis started.");
  720:     return true;
  721:   }
  722: 
  723:   function selectCandidate(candidate: Candidate) {
  724:     if (!session) return;
  725:     setCropEditingId(null);
  726:     cropBaselineRef.current = null;
  727:     patchSession({ selectedDiagramId: candidate.id });
  728:     setNotice("Region selected. The current chess position is unchanged; use Adjust Crop or Use on Board to continue.");
  729:   }
  730: 
  731:   function adjustSelectedCrop(candidate: Candidate) {
  732:     if (cropEditingId !== candidate.id) cropBaselineRef.current = candidate;
  733:     setCropEditingId(candidate.id);
  734:   }
  735: 
  736:   function resetSelectedCrop() {
  737:     const baseline = cropBaselineRef.current;
  738:     if (!baseline || !session) return;
  739:     patchSession((current) => {
  740:       const pageKey = String(current.page);
  741:       return {
  742:         ...current,
  743:         candidatesByPage: {
  744:           ...current.candidatesByPage,
  745:           [pageKey]: (current.candidatesByPage[pageKey] ?? []).map((candidate) =>
  746:             candidate.id === baseline.id ? baseline : candidate),
  747:         },
  748:       };
  749:     });
  750:   }
  751: 
  752:   function cancelCandidateSelection() {
  753:     const baseline = cropBaselineRef.current;
  754:     if (baseline && session) {
  755:       patchSession((current) => {
  756:         const pageKey = String(current.page);
  757:         return {
  758:           ...current,
  759:           candidatesByPage: {
  760:             ...current.candidatesByPage,
  761:             [pageKey]: (current.candidatesByPage[pageKey] ?? []).map((candidate) =>
  762:               candidate.id === baseline.id ? baseline : candidate),
  763:           },
  764:         };
  765:       });
  766:     }
  767:     setCropEditingId(null);
  768:     cropBaselineRef.current = null;
  769:     patchSession({ selectedDiagramId: null });
  770:   }
  771: 
  772:   async function openTransfer(candidate: Candidate) {
  773:     const canvas = canvasRef.current;
  774:     if (!canvas || canvas.width === 0 || canvas.height === 0) {
  775:       setError("The rendered page image is unavailable. Rerender the page before using this crop.");
  776:       return;
  777:     }
  778:     let preview: HTMLCanvasElement;
  779:     try {
  780:       const crop = cropToPixels(candidate, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  781:       preview = document.createElement("canvas");
  782:       preview.width = crop.width;
  783:       preview.height = crop.height;
  784:       const context = preview.getContext("2d");
  785:       if (!context) throw new Error("Could not prepare a crop preview in this browser.");
  786:       context.drawImage(canvas, crop.x, crop.y, crop.width, crop.height, 0, 0, crop.width, crop.height);
  787:     } catch (cause) {
  788:       setError(`Could not create the crop preview: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  789:       return;
  790:     }
  791:     setTransferCandidate(candidate);
  792:     setTransferPreview(preview.toDataURL("image/png"));
  793:     setTransferFen("");
  794:     setRecognitionResult(null);
  795:         setRecognitionError("");
  796:         const requestId = ++transferRequestRef.current;
  797:     setRecognitionStatus("loading");
  798:     try {
  799:       const image = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
  800:         preview.toBlob((blob) => blob ? resolve(blob) : reject(new Error("Could not encode the selected crop.")), "image/png");
  801:       });
  802:       const form = new FormData();
  803:       form.append("image", image, `board-crop-${candidate.id}.png`);
  804:       const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/recognize`, { method: "POST", body: form });
  805:       const result = await response.json() as RecognitionResult & { detail?: string };
  806:       if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.detail ?? `Recognition service returned HTTP ${response.status}.`);
  807:       if (requestId !== transferRequestRef.current) return;
  808:       setTransferFen(result.fen);
  809:       setRecognitionResult(result);
  810:       setRecognitionStatus("complete");
  811:       // A successful scan immediately becomes the active analysis position.
  812:       // Keep the review dialog open so the user can correct uncertain squares;
  813:       // the explicit confirmation button remains available for corrected FENs.
  814:       if (moveToPosition(result.fen)) {
  815:         setNotice("Scanned position loaded automatically onto the analysis board. Review the diagram and correct any uncertain squares before continuing.");
  816:       }
  817:     } catch (cause) {
  818:       if (requestId !== transferRequestRef.current) return;
  819:       setRecognitionStatus("failed");
  820:       setRecognitionError(messageOf(cause));
  821:     }
  822:   }
  823: 
  824:   function closeTransfer() {
  825:     transferRequestRef.current += 1;
  826:     setTransferCandidate(null);
  827:     setTransferPreview("");
  828:     setTransferFen("");
  829:     setRecognitionStatus("idle");
  830:     setRecognitionResult(null);
  831:     setRecognitionError("");
  832:       }
  833: 
  834: 
  835:   function updateGameEndOverlay(currentGame: Chess) {
  836:     if (currentGame.isCheckmate()) {
  837:       setGameEndOverlay({
  838:         title: "Checkmate",
  839:         message: `${currentGame.turn() === "w" ? "Black" : "White"} wins.`,
  840:       });
  841:     } else if (currentGame.isStalemate()) {
  842:       setGameEndOverlay({
  843:         title: "Stalemate",
  844:         message: "The side to move has no legal moves, but its king is not in check. The game is a draw.",
  845:       });
  846:     } else if (currentGame.isDraw()) {
  847:       let message = "The game is drawn.";
  848:       if (currentGame.isThreefoldRepetition()) message = "Draw by threefold repetition.";
  849:       else if (currentGame.isInsufficientMaterial()) message = "Draw by insufficient material.";
  850:       else if (currentGame.isDrawByFiftyMoves()) message = "Draw by the fifty-move rule.";
  851:       else if (currentGame.isDraw()) message = "Draw by a draw rule.";
  852:       setGameEndOverlay({ title: "Draw", message });
  853:     } else {
  854:       setGameEndOverlay(null);
  855:     }
  856:   }
  857: 
  858:   function completePromotion(piece: "q" | "r" | "b" | "n") {
  859:     if (!promotionRequest || !boardSession) return;
  860:     const activeBoard = boardSession;
  861:     try {
  862:       const currentGame = new Chess(activeBoard.currentFen);
  863:       const result = currentGame.move({
  864:         from: promotionRequest.from,
  865:         to: promotionRequest.to,
  866:         promotion: piece,
  867:       });
  868:       const nextMoves = [...activeBoard.moves.slice(0, activeBoard.moveIndex), result.san];
  869:       patchBoardSession({
  870:         currentFen: currentGame.fen(),
  871:         moves: nextMoves,
  872:         moveIndex: nextMoves.length,
  873:       });
  874:       updateGameEndOverlay(currentGame);
  875:       setLastMove({ from: promotionRequest.from, to: promotionRequest.to });
  876:       setSelectedSquare(null);
  877:       setPromotionRequest(null);
  878:       setError("");
  879:     } catch {
  880:       setError("Unable to complete pawn promotion.");
  881:       setPromotionRequest(null);
  882:     }
  883:   }
  884: 
  885:   function makeMove(from: Square, to: Square) {
  886:     if (!boardSession) return;
  887:     const activeBoard = boardSession;
  888:     try {
  889:       const currentGame = new Chess(activeBoard.currentFen);
  890:       const mover = currentGame.get(from);
  891:       const promote = mover?.type === "p" && (to.endsWith("8") || to.endsWith("1"));
  892:       if (promote) {
  893:         setPromotionRequest({ from, to });
  894:         return;
  895:       }
  896:       const result = currentGame.move({ from, to });
  897:       const nextMoves = [...activeBoard.moves.slice(0, activeBoard.moveIndex), result.san];
  898:       if (workspaceMode === "opening-explorer") {
  899:         const line = nextMoves
  900:           .map((move, index) => index % 2 === 0 ? `${Math.floor(index / 2) + 1}. ${move}` : move)
  901:           .join(" ");
  902:         setOpeningExplorerInput(line);
  903:         setOpeningExplorerPly(nextMoves.length);
  904:       } else {
  905:         patchBoardSession({
  906:           currentFen: currentGame.fen(),
  907:           moves: nextMoves,
  908:           moveIndex: nextMoves.length,
  909:         });
  910:       }
  911:       updateGameEndOverlay(currentGame);
  912:       setLastMove({ from, to });
  913:       setSelectedSquare(null);
  914:       setError("");
  915:     } catch {
  916:       const piece = game.get(to);
  917:       if (piece && piece.color === game.turn()) setSelectedSquare(to);
  918:       else setSelectedSquare(null);
  919:     }
  920:   }
  921: 
  922:   function handleSquareClick(square: Square) {
  923:     // A left-click removes the red highlight from that square.
  924:     setRedSquares([]);
  925: 
  926:     if (!annotationMode && (userArrows.length > 0 || arrowStart !== null)) {
  927:       setUserArrows([]);
  928:       setArrowStart(null);
  929:       setArrowHover(null);
  930:       return;
  931:     }
  932: if (!boardSession) return;
  933:     if (annotationMode) {
  934:       if (!arrowStart) {
  935:         setArrowStart(square);
  936:         return;
  937:       }
  938:       if (arrowStart !== square) setUserArrows((arrows) => toggleArrow(arrows, { from: arrowStart, to: square }));
  939:       setArrowStart(null);
  940:       setArrowHover(null);
  941:       return;
  942:     }
  943:     if (selectedSquare && legalTargets.has(square)) {
  944:       makeMove(selectedSquare, square);
  945:       return;
  946:     }
  947:     const piece = game.get(square);
  948:     if (piece?.color === game.turn()) setSelectedSquare(square);
  949:     else setSelectedSquare(null);
  950:   }
  951: 
  952:   function boardArrowPointerDown(event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
  953:   if (event.button !== 2 || !boardRef.current) return;
  954: 
  955:   event.preventDefault();
  956: 
  957:   const bounds = boardRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
  958:   const square = boardSquareAtPoint(
  959:     event.clientX,
  960:     event.clientY,
  961:     bounds,
  962:     Boolean(boardSession?.flipped),
  963:   );
  964: 
  965:   if (!square) return;
  966: 
  967:   // Only suppress the context menu if an arrow is actually drawn.
  968:   suppressBoardContextMenuRef.current = false;
  969:   arrowGestureMovedRef.current = false;
  970:   arrowGestureRef.current = square;
  971:   setArrowStart(square);
  972:   setArrowHover(square);
  973: 
  974:   event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
  975: }
  976: 
  977:   function boardArrowPointerMove(event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
  978:     if (!arrowGestureRef.current || !boardRef.current) return;
  979:     const bounds = boardRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
  980:     const square = boardSquareAtPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY, bounds, Boolean(boardSession?.flipped));
  981:     if (square && square !== arrowGestureRef.current) arrowGestureMovedRef.current = true;
  982:     setArrowHover(square);
  983:   }
  984: 
  985:   function boardArrowPointerUp(event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
  986:   const from = arrowGestureRef.current;
  987: 
  988:   if (!from || !boardRef.current) return;
  989: 
  990:   const bounds = boardRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
  991:   const to = boardSquareAtPoint(
  992:     event.clientX,
  993:     event.clientY,
  994:     bounds,
  995:     Boolean(boardSession?.flipped),
  996:   );
  997: 
  998:   if (to && to !== from) {
  999:     setUserArrows((arrows) => toggleArrow(arrows, { from, to }));
 1000:     suppressBoardContextMenuRef.current = true;
 1001:   } else {
 1002:     suppressBoardContextMenuRef.current = false;
 1003:   }
 1004: 
 1005:   arrowGestureRef.current = null;
 1006:   arrowGestureMovedRef.current = false;
 1007:   setArrowStart(null);
 1008:   setArrowHover(null);
 1009: }
 1010: 
 1011:   function cancelBoardArrowGesture() {
 1012:     arrowGestureRef.current = null;
 1013:     setArrowStart(null);
 1014:     setArrowHover(null);
 1015:   }

 1017:   function loadPgnText(source: string) {
 1018:     try {
 1019:       const imported = parsePgn(source);
 1020:       const replay = new Chess(imported.lineBaseFen);
 1021: 
 1022:       for (const san of imported.moves) {
 1023:         replay.move(san);
 1024:       }
 1025: 
 1026:       const last = replay.history({ verbose: true }).at(-1);
 1027: 
 1028:       setAnalysisBoard((current) => ({
 1029:         ...current,
 1030:         lineBaseFen: imported.lineBaseFen,
 1031:         currentFen: imported.finalFen,
 1032:         moves: imported.moves,
 1033:         moveIndex: imported.moves.length,
 1034:       }));
 1035: 
 1036:       setWorkspaceMode("analysis");
 1037:       setStudyMode(false);
 1038:       setLastMove(last ? { from: last.from, to: last.to } : null);
 1039:       setSelectedSquare(null);
 1040: 
 1041:       const white = imported.headers.White || "White";
 1042:       const black = imported.headers.Black || "Black";
 1043:       setNotice(`PGN loaded: ${white} vs ${black} � ${imported.moves.length} plies.`);
 1044:       setError("");
 1045:     } catch (cause) {
 1046:       setError(`PGN import failed: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1047:     }
 1048:   }
 1049: 
 1050:   async function importPgnFile(file: File) {
 1051:     try {
 1052:       const source = await file.text();
 1053:       loadPgnText(source);
 1054:     } catch (cause) {
 1055:       setError(`Could not read PGN file: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1056:     }
 1057:   }
 1058: 
 1059:   function pastePgn() {
 1060:     const source = window.prompt("Paste PGN notation:");
 1061:     if (source !== null) loadPgnText(source);
 1062:   }
 1063:   function jumpToMove(index: number) {
 1064:     if (!boardSession) return;
 1065:     try {
 1066:       const next = makePosition(boardSession.lineBaseFen, boardSession.moves, index);
 1067:       const last = next.history({ verbose: true }).at(-1);
 1068:       patchBoardSession({ moveIndex: index, currentFen: next.fen() });
 1069:       setLastMove(last ? { from: last.from, to: last.to } : null);
 1070:       setSelectedSquare(null);
 1071:     } catch (cause) {
 1072:       setError(`Could not navigate to move: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1073:     }
 1074:   }

 1076:   async function copyText(value: string) {
 1077:     try {
 1078:       await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
 1079:       setNotice("Copied to clipboard.");
 1080:     } catch (cause) {
 1081:       setError(`Clipboard access failed: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1082:     }
 1083:   }

 1085:   function stopBoardScan() {
 1086:     const controller = scanAbortControllerRef.current;
 1087:     if (!controller) return;
 1088: 
 1089:     ++scanRequestIdRef.current;
 1090:     scanAbortControllerRef.current = null;
 1091:     controller.abort();
 1092:     setDetecting(false);
 1093:     setNotice("Scan cancelled.");
 1094: 
 1095:     patchSession((current) => ({
 1096:       ...current,
 1097:       candidatesByPage: {
 1098:         ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1099:         [String(current.page)]: [],
 1100:       },
 1101:       selectedDiagramId: null,
 1102:     }));
 1103:   }
 1104: 
 1105: 
 1106:   function clearBoardScan() {
 1107:     // Invalidate any pending response before aborting the request.
 1108:     ++scanRequestIdRef.current;
 1109: 
 1110:     const controller = scanAbortControllerRef.current;
 1111:     scanAbortControllerRef.current = null;
 1112:     controller?.abort();
 1113: 
 1114:     setDetecting(false);
 1115:     setManualMode(false);
 1116:     setDragSelection(null);
 1117:     setCropEditingId(null);
 1118:     cropEditStartRef.current = null;
 1119:     dragStartRef.current = null;
 1120: 
 1121:     patchSession((current) => ({
 1122:       ...current,
 1123:       candidatesByPage: {
 1124:         ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1125:         [String(current.page)]: [],
 1126:       },
 1127:       selectedDiagramId: null,
 1128:     }));
 1129: 
 1130:     setError("");
 1131:     setNotice("Board detection cleared.");
 1132:   }
 1133:   async function detectBoards() {
 1134:     if (detecting) {
 1135:       stopBoardScan();
 1136:       return;
 1137:     }
 1138: 
 1139:     if (!canvasRef.current || !session) return;
 1140:     if (!API_BASE) {
 1141:       setError("Board detection API is not configured. Configure VITE_API_BASE_URL and start the optional CV service.");
 1142:       return;
 1143:     }
 1144: 
 1145:     scanAbortControllerRef.current?.abort();
 1146:     const abortController = new AbortController();
 1147:     scanAbortControllerRef.current = abortController;
 1148:     const requestId = ++scanRequestIdRef.current;
 1149: 
 1150:     setDetecting(true);
 1151:     setError("");
 1152:     setNotice("Scanning current page...");
 1153:     patchSession((current) => ({
 1154:       ...current,
 1155:       candidatesByPage: {
 1156:         ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1157:         [String(current.page)]: [],
 1158:       },
 1159:       selectedDiagramId: null,
 1160:     }));
 1161: 
 1162:     try {
 1163:       const image = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
 1164:         canvasRef.current?.toBlob(
 1165:           (blob) => blob
 1166:             ? resolve(blob)
 1167:             : reject(new Error("Could not encode rendered PDF page.")),
 1168:           "image/png",
 1169:         );
 1170:       });
 1171: 
 1172:       if (requestId !== scanRequestIdRef.current) return;
 1173: 
 1174:       const form = new FormData();
 1175:       form.append("image", image, `page-${session.page}.png`);
 1176: 
 1177:       const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/detect`, {
 1178:         method: "POST",
 1179:         body: form,
 1180:         signal: abortController.signal,
 1181:       });
 1182: 
 1183:       if (!response.ok) {
 1184:         throw new Error(`Detector returned HTTP ${response.status}.`);
 1185:       }
 1186: 
 1187:       const data = await response.json() as { candidates?: CandidateInput[] };
 1188:       const candidates = (data.candidates ?? []).map(
 1189:         (item, index) => normalizedCandidate({ ...item, source: "automatic" }, index),
 1190:       );
 1191: 
 1192:       if (requestId !== scanRequestIdRef.current) return;
 1193: 
 1194:       patchSession((current) => ({
 1195:         ...current,
 1196:         candidatesByPage: {
 1197:           ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1198:           [String(current.page)]: candidates,
 1199:         },
 1200:         selectedDiagramId: null,
 1201:       }));
 1202: 
 1203:       setNotice(
 1204:         candidates.length
 1205:           ? `Found ${candidates.length} board candidate${candidates.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}. Select a candidate to recognize its chess position and load it onto the analysis board.`
 1206:           : "No board candidates found.",
 1207:       );
 1208:     } catch (cause) {
 1209:       if (requestId !== scanRequestIdRef.current) return;
 1210: 
 1211:       if (abortController.signal.aborted) {
 1212:         setNotice("Scan cancelled.");
 1213:       } else {
 1214:         setError(`Board detection failed: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1215:       }
 1216:     } finally {
 1217:       if (requestId === scanRequestIdRef.current) {
 1218:         scanAbortControllerRef.current = null;
 1219:         setDetecting(false);
 1220:       }
 1221:     }
 1222:   }
 1223:   function pointerPosition(event: { clientX: number; clientY: number }) {
 1224:     const box = canvasRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
 1225:     return box ? pointOnCanvas(event.clientX, event.clientY, box) : null;
 1226:   }
 1227: 
 1228:   function startCropEdit(
 1229:     event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLElement>,
 1230:     candidate: Candidate,
 1231:     handle: CropHandle,
 1232:   ) {
 1233:     event.stopPropagation();
 1234:     const point = pointerPosition(event);
 1235:     const stage = pageStageRef.current;
 1236:     if (!point || !stage) return;
 1237:     cropEditStartRef.current = { candidate, handle, point };
 1238:     stage.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
 1239:   }
 1240: 
 1241:   function startRegion(event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
 1242:     if (cropEditStartRef.current) return;
 1243:     if (!manualMode) return;
 1244: const canvas = canvasRef.current;
 1245: if (!canvas) return;
 1246: const target = event.target as HTMLElement;
 1247: if (target.closest(".diagram-overlay, .crop-handle")) return;
 1248: const bounds = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
 1249: if (
 1250:   event.clientX < bounds.left ||
 1251:   event.clientX > bounds.right ||
 1252:   event.clientY < bounds.top ||
 1253:   event.clientY > bounds.bottom
 1254: ) return;
 1255:     const point = pointerPosition(event);
 1256:     if (!point) return;
 1257:     dragStartRef.current = point;
 1258:     const selection = { ...point, x2: point.x, y2: point.y };
 1259:     dragCurrentRef.current = selection;
 1260:     setDragSelection(selection);
 1261:     event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
 1262:   }
 1263: 
 1264:   function moveRegion(event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
 1265:     const edit = cropEditStartRef.current;
 1266:     if (edit) {
 1267:       const point = pointerPosition(event);
 1268:       if (!point || !session) return;
 1269:       const next = editCrop(edit.candidate, edit.handle, point.x - edit.point.x, point.y - edit.point.y);
 1270:       patchSession((current) => {
 1271:         const pageKey = String(current.page);
 1272:         return {
 1273:           ...current,
 1274:           candidatesByPage: {
 1275:             ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1276:             [pageKey]: (current.candidatesByPage[pageKey] ?? []).map((candidate) =>
 1277:               candidate.id === edit.candidate.id ? { ...candidate, ...next } : candidate),
 1278:           },
 1279:         };
 1280:       });
 1281:       return;
 1282:     }
 1283:     if (!dragStartRef.current) return;
 1284:     const point = pointerPosition(event);
 1285:     if (point) {
 1286:       const selection = { ...dragStartRef.current, x2: point.x, y2: point.y };
 1287:       dragCurrentRef.current = selection;
 1288:       setDragSelection(selection);
 1289:     }
 1290:   }
 1291: 
 1292:   function finishRegion() {
 1293:     if (cropEditStartRef.current) {
 1294:       cropEditStartRef.current = null;
 1295:       return;
 1296:     }
 1297:     const selection = dragCurrentRef.current;
 1298:     if (!dragStartRef.current || !selection || !session) return;
 1299:     const x = Math.min(selection.x, selection.x2);
 1300:     const y = Math.min(selection.y, selection.y2);
 1301:     const width = Math.abs(selection.x2 - selection.x);
 1302:     const height = Math.abs(selection.y2 - selection.y);
 1303:     dragStartRef.current = null;
 1304:     dragCurrentRef.current = null;
 1305:     setDragSelection(null);
 1306:     if (width < 0.025 || height < 0.025) return;
 1307:     const candidate: Candidate = {
 1308:       id: `manual-${crypto.randomUUID()}`,
 1309:       x, y, width, height,
 1310:       relativeScore: 1,
 1311:       source: "manual",
 1312:     };
 1313:     patchSession((current) => ({
 1314:       ...current,
 1315:       candidatesByPage: {
 1316:         ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1317:         [String(current.page)]: [...(current.candidatesByPage[String(current.page)] ?? []), candidate],
 1318:       },
 1319:       selectedDiagramId: candidate.id,
 1320:     }));
 1321:     setManualMode(false);
 1322:     setNotice("Manual crop selected. Adjust its boundary, then use it on the board when ready.");
 1323:   }
 1324: 
 1325:   function cancelRegion() {
 1326:     cropEditStartRef.current = null;
 1327:     dragStartRef.current = null;
 1328:     dragCurrentRef.current = null;
 1329:     setDragSelection(null);
 1330:   }
 1331: 
 1332:   async function fitPage(mode: "width" | "page") {
 1333:     if (!pdf || !session || !pageStageRef.current) return;
 1334:     try {
 1335:       const page = await pdf.getPage(session.page);
 1336:       const base = page.getViewport({ scale: 1 });
 1337:       const renderScale = Math.min(1.4, 1100 / base.width);
 1338:       const availableWidth = Math.max(200, pageStageRef.current.parentElement?.clientWidth ?? base.width) - 44;
 1339:       const availableHeight = Math.max(250, pageStageRef.current.parentElement?.clientHeight ?? base.height) - 52;
 1340:       const scale = mode === "width"
 1341:         ? availableWidth / (base.width * renderScale)
 1342:         : Math.min(availableWidth / base.width, availableHeight / base.height) / renderScale;
 1343:       patchSession({ zoom: Math.max(0.45, Math.min(1.8, scale)) });
 1344:     } catch (cause) {
 1345:       setError(`Could not fit PDF page: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1346:     }
 1347:   }

 1350: 
 1351:   function saveBookmark() {
 1352:     if (!session) return;
 1353:     patchSession((current) => ({
 1354:       ...current,
 1355:       bookmarks: current.bookmarks.includes(current.page)
 1356:         ? current.bookmarks.filter((page) => page !== current.page)
 1357:         : [...current.bookmarks, current.page].sort((a, b) => a - b),
 1358:     }));
 1359:   }

 1361:   function savePosition() {

 1363:     const position = {
 1364:       id: crypto.randomUUID(),

 1366:       fen: session.currentFen,
 1367:       page: session.page,
 1368:       savedAt: Date.now(),
 1369:     };
 1370:     patchSession((current) => ({ ...current, positions: [...current.positions, position] }));
 1371:     setNotice("Position saved to this book.");
 1372:   }

 1594:                     const to = squareCenter(arrow.to, Boolean(boardSession?.flipped));
 1595:                     const dx = to.x - from.x;
 1596:                     const dy = to.y - from.y;
 1597:                     const length = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
 1598:                     if (length < 0.001) return null;
 1599: 
 1600:                     const ux = dx / length;
 1601:                     const uy = dy / length;
 1602:                     const px = -uy;
 1603:                     const py = ux;
 1604: 
 1605:                     const headLength = Math.min(0.34, length * 0.30);
 1606:                     const headHalfWidth = 0.19;
 1607:                     const shaftHalfWidth = kind === "preview" ? 0.045 : 0.055;
 1608:                     const tipX = to.x - ux * 0.04;
 1609:                     const tipY = to.y - uy * 0.04;
 1610:                     const baseX = tipX - ux * headLength;
 1611:                     const baseY = tipY - uy * headLength;
 1612:                     const startX = from.x + ux * 0.12;
 1613:                     const startY = from.y + uy * 0.12;
 1614: 
 1615:                     const points = [
 1616:                       [startX + px * shaftHalfWidth, startY + py * shaftHalfWidth],
 1617:                       [baseX + px * shaftHalfWidth, baseY + py * shaftHalfWidth],
 1618:                       [baseX + px * headHalfWidth, baseY + py * headHalfWidth],
 1619:                       [tipX, tipY],
 1620:                       [baseX - px * headHalfWidth, baseY - py * headHalfWidth],
 1621:                       [baseX - px * shaftHalfWidth, baseY - py * shaftHalfWidth],
 1622:                       [startX - px * shaftHalfWidth, startY - py * shaftHalfWidth],
 1623:                     ];
 1624:                     const color = kind === "engine" ? "#f0a23b" : "#81b64c";

 1626:

 1629:                         key={`${kind}-${arrow.from}-${arrow.to}-${index}`}

 1632:                         opacity={opacity}

 1634:                       />
 1635:                     );

 2037: function LibraryNavItem({
 2038:   active,
 2039:   onClick,
 2040:   icon: Icon,
 2041:   label,
 2042: }: {
 2043:   active: boolean;
 2044:   onClick: () => void;
 2045:   icon: React.ComponentType<{ size?: number; strokeWidth?: number; "aria-hidden"?: boolean }>;
 2046:   label: string;
 2047: }) {
 2048:   return (
 2049:     <button
 2050:       type="button"
 2051:       className={`library-nav-item ${active ? "nav-active" : ""}`}
 2052:       onClick={onClick}
 2053:       aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
 2054:     >
 2055:       <Icon size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} aria-hidden={true} />
 2056:       <span>{label}</span>
 2057:     </button>
 2058:   );
 2059: }
 2060: 
 2061: function formatSize(bytes: number): string {
 2062:   if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${Math.max(1, Math.round(bytes / 1024))} KB`;
 2063:   return `${(bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`;
 2064: }
 2065: 
 2066: function isTypingTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
 2067:   return target instanceof HTMLElement && (target.isContentEditable || ["INPUT", "TEXTAREA", "SELECT"].includes(target.tagName));
 2068: }

    1: import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
    2: import { START_FEN } from "./model";
    3: import { fenWithTurn, makePosition } from "./chess-state";
    4: 
    5: describe("chess position state", () => {
    6:   it("replays legal SAN from an arbitrary diagram position", () => {
    7:     const game = makePosition(START_FEN, ["e4", "e5"], 2);
    8:     expect(game.fen()).toContain(" w KQkq ");
    9:     expect(game.history()).toEqual(["e4", "e5"]);
   10:   });
   11: 
   12:   it("rejects invalid restored moves instead of substituting a position", () => {
   13:     expect(() => makePosition(START_FEN, ["e5"], 1)).toThrow();
   14:   });
   15: 
   16:   it("changes side to move and clears en-passant metadata", () => {
   17:     expect(fenWithTurn("8/8/8/8/8/8/4k3/4K3 w - e6 0 1", "b"))
   18:       .toBe("8/8/8/8/8/8/4k3/4K3 b - - 0 1");
   19:   });
   20: });


==================================================
FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\chess-state.ts
==================================================
    1: import { Chess } from "chess.js";
    2: 
    3: export function makePosition(baseFen: string, moves: string[], moveIndex: number): Chess {
    4:   const game = new Chess(baseFen);
    5:   for (const san of moves.slice(0, moveIndex)) {
    6:     const result = game.move(san);
    7:     if (!result) throw new Error(`Saved move could not be replayed: ${san}`);
    8:   }
    9:   return game;
   10: }
   11: 
   12: export function fenWithTurn(fen: string, turn: "w" | "b"): string {
   13:   const fields = fen.split(/\s+/);
   14:   if (fields.length !== 6) throw new Error("FEN must contain six fields.");
   15:   fields[1] = turn;
   16:   fields[3] = "-";
   17:   fields[4] = "0";
   18:   return fields.join(" ");
   19: }

FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\CoordinateTraining.tsx

    1: import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
    2: import "./coordinate-training.css";
    3: 
    4: type Mode = "findSquare" | "nameSquare";
    5: type Orientation = "white" | "black";
    6: type Session = { date: string; score: number; mode: Mode; orientation: Orientation };
    7: 
    8: const FILES = "abcdefgh";
    9: const RANKS = "12345678";
   10: const KEY = "chessbook-coordinate-training-v2";
   11: const PIECES = [
   12:   ["br","bn","bb","bq","bk","bb","bn","br"],
   13:   ["bp","bp","bp","bp","bp","bp","bp","bp"],
   14:   ["","","","","","","",""],
   15:   ["","","","","","","",""],
   16:   ["","","","","","","",""],
   17:   ["","","","","","","",""],
   18:   ["wp","wp","wp","wp","wp","wp","wp","wp"],
   19:   ["wr","wn","wb","wq","wk","wb","wn","wr"],
   20: ];
   21: 
   22: function nextSquare(previous: string, avoidLine = true) {
   23:   let choices: string[] = [];
   24:   for (const file of FILES) for (const rank of RANKS) {
   25:     if (!avoidLine || (file !== previous[0] && rank !== previous[1])) choices.push(file + rank);
   26:   }
   27:   return choices[Math.floor(Math.random() * choices.length)];
   28: }
   29: 
   30: function readHistory(): Session[] {
   31:   try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || "[]"); }
   32:   catch { return []; }
   33: }
   34: 
   35: export default function CoordinateTraining() {
   36:   const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("findSquare");
   37:   const [orientation, setOrientation] = useState<Orientation>("white");
   38:   const [timed, setTimed] = useState(true);
   39:   const [showCoordinates, setShowCoordinates] = useState(true);
   40:   const [showPieces, setShowPieces] = useState(true);
   41:   const [sound, setSound] = useState(false);
   42:   const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
   43:   const [remaining, setRemaining] = useState(30);
   44:   const [target, setTarget] = useState(() => nextSquare(""));
   45:   const [nextTarget, setNextTarget] = useState(() => nextSquare(""));
   46:   const [score, setScore] = useState(0);
   47:   const [attempts, setAttempts] = useState(0);
   48:   const [correct, setCorrect] = useState(0);
   49:   const [streak, setStreak] = useState(0);
   50:   const [bestStreak, setBestStreak] = useState(0);
   51:   const [wrong, setWrong] = useState(false);
   52:   const [history, setHistory] = useState<Session[]>(readHistory);
   53:   const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
   54:   const [started, setStarted] = useState(false);
   55:   const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   56:   const wrongTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
   57:   const audioRef = useRef<AudioContext | null>(null);
   58:   const accuracy = attempts ? Math.round((correct / attempts) * 100) : 100;
   59:   const finished = timed && remaining <= 0;
   60: 
   61:   const playTone = (good: boolean) => {
   62:     if (!sound) return;
   63:     try {
   64:       const AudioContextClass = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
   65:       if (!audioRef.current) audioRef.current = new AudioContextClass();
   66:       const ctx = audioRef.current;
   67:       const oscillator = ctx.createOscillator();
   68:       const gain = ctx.createGain();
   69:       oscillator.frequency.value = good ? 660 : 180;
   70:       gain.gain.value = 0.06;
   71:       oscillator.connect(gain);
   72:       gain.connect(ctx.destination);
   73:       oscillator.start();
   74:       oscillator.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.09);
   75:     } catch { /* Audio is optional */ }
   76:   };
   77: 
   78:   const saveSession = () => {
   79:     if (!started) return;
   80:     const entry: Session = { date: new Date().toISOString(), score, mode, orientation };
   81:     setHistory(previous => {
   82:       const next = [entry, ...previous].slice(0, 20);
   83:       try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(next)); } catch { /* Storage unavailable */ }
   84:       return next;
   85:     });
   86:     setStarted(false);
   87:   };
   88: 
   89:   const start = () => {
   90:     if (wrongTimer.current) clearTimeout(wrongTimer.current);
   91:     setScore(0);
   92:     setAttempts(0);
   93:     setCorrect(0);
   94:     setStreak(0);
   95:     setBestStreak(0);
   96:     setRemaining(30);
   97:     setWrong(false);
   98:     setAnswer("");
   99:     const first = nextSquare("");
  100:     setTarget(first);
  101:     setNextTarget(nextSquare(first));
  102:     setStarted(true);
  103:     setPlaying(true);
  104:     if (mode === "nameSquare") setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 0);
  105:   };
  106: 
  107:   const stop = () => {
  108:     setPlaying(false);
  109:     saveSession();
  110:   };
  111: 
  112:   useEffect(() => {
  113:     if (!playing || !timed) return;
  114:     const startAt = Date.now();
  115:     const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
  116:       const left = Math.max(0, 30 - Math.floor((Date.now() - startAt) / 1000));
  117:       setRemaining(left);
  118:       if (left === 0) {
  119:         window.clearInterval(timer);
  120:         setPlaying(false);
  121:       }
  122:     }, 100);
  123:     return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  124:   }, [playing, timed]);
  125: 
  126:   useEffect(() => {
  127:     if (finished && started) saveSession();
  128:   }, [finished]);
  129: 
  130:   useEffect(() => () => {
  131:     if (wrongTimer.current) clearTimeout(wrongTimer.current);
  132:     if (audioRef.current) void audioRef.current.close();
  133:   }, []);
  134: 
  135:   const markWrong = () => {
  136:     setAttempts(v => v + 1);
  137:     setStreak(0);
  138:     setWrong(true);
  139:     playTone(false);
  140:     if (wrongTimer.current) clearTimeout(wrongTimer.current);
  141:     wrongTimer.current = setTimeout(() => setWrong(false), 500);
  142:   };
  143: 
  144:   const markCorrect = () => {
  145:     setAttempts(v => v + 1);
  146:     setCorrect(v => v + 1);
  147:     setScore(v => v + 1);
  148:     setStreak(v => {
  149:       const next = v + 1;
  150:       setBestStreak(best => Math.max(best, next));
  151:       return next;
  152:     });
  153:     setWrong(false);
  154:     playTone(true);
  155:     setTarget(nextTarget);
  156:     setNextTarget(nextSquare(nextTarget));
  157:     setAnswer("");
  158:   };
  159: 
  160:   const chooseSquare = (square: string) => {
  161:     if (!playing || mode !== "findSquare") return;
  162:     if (square === target) markCorrect();
  163:     else markWrong();
  164:   };
  165: 
  166:   const submitAnswer = (value: string) => {
  167:     if (!playing || mode !== "nameSquare") return;
  168:     const normalized = value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-h1-8]/g, "").slice(0, 2);
  169:     setAnswer(normalized);
  170:     if (normalized.length === 2) {
  171:       if (/^[a-h][1-8]$/.test(normalized) && normalized === target) markCorrect();
  172:       else if (/^[a-h][1-8]$/.test(normalized)) {
  173:         markWrong();
  174:         setAnswer("");
  175:       }
  176:     }
  177:   };
  178: 
  179:   const files = orientation === "white" ? [...FILES] : [...FILES].reverse();
  180:   const ranks = orientation === "white" ? [...RANKS].reverse() : [...RANKS];
  181: 
  182:   return (
  183:     <main className="coordinate-training-workspace">
  184:       <header className="ct-topbar">
  185:         <div className="ct-brand">
  186:           <span className="ct-brand-mark"><span className="ct-grass-icon" aria-hidden="true"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span></span>
  187:           <div><strong>Coordinate Training</strong><small>Improve your board vision</small></div>
  188:         </div>
  189:         <div className="ct-top-actions">
  190:           <button type="button" onClick={() => setOrientation(v => v === "white" ? "black" : "white")}>? Flip board</button>
  191:           {playing
  192:             ? <button type="button" className="ct-start-button" onClick={stop}>Stop training</button>
  193:             : <button type="button" className="ct-start-button" onClick={start}>Start training</button>}
  194:         </div>
  195:       </header>
  196: 
  197:       <div className="ct-main">
  198:         <section className="ct-play-area">
  199:           <div className="ct-mode-tabs">
  200:             <button className={mode === "findSquare" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => { setMode("findSquare"); setPlaying(false); setStarted(false); }}>Find the square</button>
  201:             <button className={mode === "nameSquare" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => { setMode("nameSquare"); setPlaying(false); setStarted(false); }}>Name the square</button>
  202:           </div>
  203: 
  204:           <div className="ct-instruction">
  205:             <span>{mode === "findSquare" ? "Find this square" : "Which square is highlighted?"}</span>
  206:             <small className={wrong ? "ct-feedback-error" : ""}>
  207:               {!playing ? (finished ? "Time is up!" : "Press Start training to begin") : wrong ? "Not quite � try again" : "Select the correct square"}
  208:             </small>
  209:           </div>
  210: 
  211:           <div className={`ct-board-frame ${wrong ? "is-wrong" : ""}`}>
  212:             <div className="ct-board" role="group" aria-label="Coordinate training chessboard">
  213:               {ranks.flatMap((rank, row) => files.map((file, col) => {
  214:                 const square = file + rank;
  215:                 const light = (FILES.indexOf(file) + Number(rank)) % 2 === 1;
  216:                 const isHighlighted = mode === "nameSquare" && playing && square === target;
  217:                 const piece = PIECES[8 - Number(rank)][FILES.indexOf(file)];
  218:                 const showRank = mode === "findSquare" && showCoordinates && (orientation === "white" ? col === 0 : col === 7);
  219:                 const showFile = mode === "findSquare" && showCoordinates && (orientation === "white" ? row === 7 : row === 0);
  220:                 return (
  221:                   <button
  222:                     key={square}
  223:                     type="button"
  224:                     aria-label={square}
  225:                     disabled={!playing || mode !== "findSquare"}
  226:                     onClick={() => chooseSquare(square)}
  227:                     className={`ct-square ${light ? "light" : "dark"} ${isHighlighted ? "ct-highlight" : ""}`}
  228:                   >
  229:                     {showPieces && piece && <img draggable="false" alt="" src={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}assets/pieces/standalone/${piece}.svg`} />}
  230:                     {showRank && <span className="ct-coordinate ct-rank">{rank}</span>}
  231:                     {showFile && <span className="ct-coordinate ct-file">{file}</span>}
  232:                   </button>
  233:                 );
  234:               }))}
  235:               {playing && mode === "findSquare" && (
  236:                 <div className="ct-board-targets" aria-hidden="true">
  237:                   <span className="ct-board-current">{mode === "findSquare" ? target : target}</span>
  238:                   <span className="ct-board-next">{nextTarget}</span>
  239:                 </div>
  240:               )}
  241:             </div>
  242:           </div>
  243: 
  244:           {mode === "nameSquare" && (
  245:             <form className="ct-answer-form" onSubmit={e => { e.preventDefault(); if (answer.length === 2) submitAnswer(answer); }}>
  246:               <input
  247:                 ref={inputRef}
  248:                 value={answer}
  249:                 disabled={!playing}
  250:                 onChange={e => submitAnswer(e.target.value)}
  251:                 onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === "Enter") e.preventDefault(); }}
  252:                 placeholder="Type square (e.g. e4)"
  253:                 maxLength={2}
  254:                 autoComplete="off"
  255:                 aria-label="Enter square coordinate"
  256:               />
  257:             </form>
  258:           )}
  259: 
  260:           <div className="ct-board-footer">
  261:             <span>{orientation === "white" ? "White perspective" : "Black perspective"}</span>
  262:             <span>{playing ? "Training in progress" : "Ready when you are"}</span>
  263:           </div>
  264:         </section>
  265: 
  266:         <aside className="ct-sidebar">
  267:           <section className="ct-panel ct-score-panel">
  268:             <div className="ct-panel-title">YOUR SESSION</div>
  269:             <div className="ct-clock">{timed ? `00:${String(remaining).padStart(2, "0")}` : "8"}</div>
  270:             <div className="ct-time-track"><span style={{ width: `${timed ? (remaining / 30) * 100 : 100}%` }} /></div>
  271:             <div className="ct-score-grid">
  272:               <div><strong>{score}</strong><small>Score</small></div>
  273:               <div><strong>{accuracy}%</strong><small>Accuracy</small></div>
  274:               <div><strong>{streak}</strong><small>Streak</small></div>
  275:               <div><strong>{bestStreak}</strong><small>Best streak</small></div>
  276:             </div>

  279:           </section>

  282:             <div className="ct-panel-title">SETTINGS</div>

  284:               <span>Time control</span>
  285:               <select value={timed ? "timed" : "untimed"} onChange={e => { setTimed(e.target.value === "timed"); setPlaying(false); setStarted(false); setRemaining(30); }}>
  286:                 <option value="timed">30 seconds</option>
  287:                 <option value="untimed">Unlimited</option>
  288:               </select>
  289:             </label>

  291:             <label className="ct-setting-toggle"><span>Show pieces</span><input type="checkbox" checked={showPieces} onChange={e => setShowPieces(e.target.checked)} /></label>
  292:             <label className="ct-setting-toggle"><span>Sound</span><input type="checkbox" checked={sound} onChange={e => setSound(e.target.checked)} /></label>
  293:             <button className="ct-start-button ct-wide-button" onClick={playing ? stop : start}>{playing ? "Stop training" : "Start training"}</button>
  294:           </section>
  295: 
  296:           <section className="ct-panel ct-history-panel">
  297:             <div className="ct-panel-title">RECENT SCORES</div>

  307:           </section>

   16: function evaluationText(score?: { kind: "cp" | "mate"; value: number }) {
   17:   if (!score) return "�";
   18:   if (score.kind === "mate") return `${score.value > 0 ? "+" : ""}#${Math.abs(score.value)}`;
   19:   const value = score.value / 100;
   20:   return `${value > 0 ? "+" : ""}${value.toFixed(2)}`;
   21: }

   23: function resultText(result?: string) {
   24:   if (result === "1-0") return "White won";
   25:   if (result === "0-1") return "Black won";
   26:   if (result === "1/2-1/2") return "Draw";
   27:   return "Result not recorded";
   28: }

   38: export function GameReview() {

   51:   const runToken = useRef(0);
   52: 
   53:   useEffect(() => {
   54:     return () => {
   55:       runToken.current += 1;
   56:       engineRef.current?.dispose();
   57:       engineRef.current = null;
   58:     };
   59:   }, []);
   60: 
   61:   function loadPgn(text: string) {
   62:     try {
   63:       const parsed = reconstructReviewGame(text);
   64:       runToken.current += 1;
   65:       engineRef.current?.dispose();
   66:       engineRef.current = null;

   68:       setReview(parsed);
   69:       setAnalysis(null);

   79:     }
   80:   }
   81: 
   82:   async function handleFile(event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
   83:     const file = event.target.files?.[0];
   84:     event.target.value = "";

   86:     try {
   87:       const text = await file.text();
   88:       loadPgn(text);
   89:     } catch {

   91:       setStatus("error");
   92:     }
   93:   }
   94: 
   95:   async function startAnalysis() {
   96:     if (!review || status === "running") return;
   97: 
   98:     const token = ++runToken.current;
   99:     engineRef.current?.dispose();
  100:     engineRef.current = null;
  101:     setAnalysis(null);
  102:     setProgress({ completed: 0, total: review.positions.length + 1, currentPly: 0 });
  103:     setError("");

  110: 
  111:       const result = await analyzeReviewPositions(review, engine, {
  112:         depth,
  113:         onProgress: value => {
  114:           if (runToken.current === token) setProgress(value);
  115:         }
  116:       });
  117: 
  118:       if (runToken.current !== token) return;
  119:       setAnalysis(result);
  120:       setStatus("complete");
  121:       setProgress(null);
  122:       engine.dispose();
  123:       engineRef.current = null;
  124:     } catch (cause) {
  125:       if (runToken.current !== token) return;
  126:       engineRef.current = null;
  127:       const message = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Stockfish analysis failed.";
  128:       if (/cancelled|disposed/i.test(message)) {

  130:       } else {

  132:         setError(message);

  135:     }
  136:   }

  143:     setStatus(review ? "ready" : "empty");
  144:   }
