    1: import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    2: import {
    3:   buildFullFen,
    4:   countKings,
    5:   EMPTY_PLACEMENT,
    6:   flipFenPlacement,
    7:   moveFenPiece,
    8:   parseFenPlacement,
    9:   PIECE_LABELS,
   10:   sanitizeCastlingRights,
   11:   setFenPiece,
   12:   START_PLACEMENT,
   13:   validatePositionFen,
   14: } from "./position-editor";
   15: 
   16: const WHITE_PIECES = ["K", "Q", "R", "B", "N", "P"] as const;
   17: const BLACK_PIECES = ["k", "q", "r", "b", "n", "p"] as const;
   18: 
   19: export interface ManualBoardEditorProps {
   20:   initialFen: string;
   21:   onConfirm: (fen: string) => void;
   22:   onCancel: () => void;
   23:   confirmLabel: string;
   24:   title: string;
   25:   subtitle?: string;
   26:   // Specific to recognition review (Feature A)
   27:   cropPreviewUrl?: string;
   28:   recognitionStatus?: "idle" | "loading" | "complete" | "failed";
   29:   recognitionResult?: {
   30:     board_confidence: number;
   31:     mean_piece_confidence: number | null;
   32:     piece_count: number;
   33:     orientation: "normal" | "flipped" | "unknown";
   34:     warnings: string[];
   35:   } | null;
   36:   recognitionError?: string;
   37:   onRetryRecognition?: () => void;
   38:   // Specific to setup mode (Feature B)
   39:   showPresets?: boolean;
   40:   currentAnalysisFen?: string;
   41: }
   42: 
   43: export function ManualBoardEditor({
   44:   initialFen,
   45:   onConfirm,
   46:   onCancel,
   47:   confirmLabel,
   48:   title,
   49:   subtitle,
   50:   cropPreviewUrl,
   51:   recognitionStatus,
   52:   recognitionResult,
   53:   recognitionError,
   54:   onRetryRecognition,
   55:   showPresets = false,
   56:   currentAnalysisFen,
   57: }: ManualBoardEditorProps) {
   58:   // Parse initial fields
   59:   const parsedInitial = useMemo(() => {
   60:     const trimmed = initialFen.trim();
   61:     const fields = trimmed ? trimmed.split(/\s+/) : [];
   62:     const placement = fields[0] && parseFenPlacement(fields[0]) ? fields[0] : START_PLACEMENT;
   63:     const turn = fields[1] === "b" ? ("b" as const) : ("w" as const);
   64:     const castling = fields[2] ?? "-";
   65:     const ep = fields[3] ?? "-";
   66:     const halfmove = Number(fields[4]) || 0;
   67:     const fullmove = Number(fields[5]) || 1;
   68:     const fen = buildFullFen(placement, turn, castling, ep, halfmove, fullmove);
   69:     return { placement, turn, castling, ep, halfmove, fullmove, fen };
   70:   }, [initialFen]);
   71: 
   72:   const [placement, setPlacement] = useState(parsedInitial.placement);
   73:   const [turn, setTurn] = useState<"w" | "b">(parsedInitial.turn);
   74:   const [castling, setCastling] = useState(parsedInitial.castling);
   75:   const [activeTool, setActiveTool] = useState<string>("P"); // Piece symbol or "" for erase
   76:   const [selectedSquare, setSelectedSquare] = useState<{ row: number; col: number } | null>(null);
   77:   const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState(false);
   78:   const [history, setHistory] = useState<string[]>([parsedInitial.fen]);
   79:   const [historyIndex, setHistoryIndex] = useState(0);
   80:   const [fenInputText, setFenInputText] = useState(parsedInitial.fen);
   81:   const [validationError, setValidationError] = useState<string>("");
   82: 
   83:   // When initialFen changes externally (e.g. recognition completes or retries), reset editor
   84:   useEffect(() => {
   85:     setPlacement(parsedInitial.placement);
   86:     setTurn(parsedInitial.turn);
   87:     setCastling(parsedInitial.castling);
   88:     setHistory([parsedInitial.fen]);
   89:     setHistoryIndex(0);
   90:     setFenInputText(parsedInitial.fen);
   91:     setValidationError("");
   92:     setSelectedSquare(null);
   93:   }, [parsedInitial]);
   94: 
   95:   const currentBoard = useMemo(() => parseFenPlacement(placement) ?? parseFenPlacement(EMPTY_PLACEMENT)!, [placement]);
   96:   const currentFen = useMemo(() => buildFullFen(placement, turn, castling), [placement, turn, castling]);
   97:   const validation = useMemo(() => validatePositionFen(currentFen), [currentFen]);
   98:   const kingCounts = useMemo(() => countKings(placement), [placement]);
   99: 
  100:   // Push new state to history
  101:   function pushState(newPlacement: string, newTurn: "w" | "b" = turn, newCastling: string = castling) {
  102:     const board = parseFenPlacement(newPlacement);
  103:     const sanitizedCastling = board ? sanitizeCastlingRights(board, newCastling) : "-";
  104:     const newFen = buildFullFen(newPlacement, newTurn, sanitizedCastling);
  105: 
  106:     setPlacement(newPlacement);
  107:     setTurn(newTurn);
  108:     setCastling(sanitizedCastling);
  109:     setFenInputText(newFen);
  110:     setValidationError("");
  111: 
  112:     if (newFen !== history[historyIndex]) {
  113:       const nextHistory = history.slice(0, historyIndex + 1);
  114:       nextHistory.push(newFen);
  115:       setHistory(nextHistory);
  116:       setHistoryIndex(nextHistory.length - 1);
  117:     }
  118:   }
  119: 
  120:   function handleUndo() {
  121:     if (historyIndex > 0) {
  122:       const prevIndex = historyIndex - 1;
  123:       const prevFen = history[prevIndex];
  124:       const fields = prevFen.split(/\s+/);
  125:       setPlacement(fields[0]);
  126:       setTurn(fields[1] === "b" ? "b" : "w");
  127:       setCastling(fields[2] ?? "-");
  128:       setFenInputText(prevFen);
  129:       setHistoryIndex(prevIndex);
  130:       setValidationError("");
  131:     }
  132:   }
  133: 
  134:   function handleRedo() {
  135:     if (historyIndex < history.length - 1) {
  136:       const nextIndex = historyIndex + 1;
  137:       const nextFen = history[nextIndex];
  138:       const fields = nextFen.split(/\s+/);
  139:       setPlacement(fields[0]);
  140:       setTurn(fields[1] === "b" ? "b" : "w");
  141:       setCastling(fields[2] ?? "-");
  142:       setFenInputText(nextFen);
  143:       setHistoryIndex(nextIndex);
  144:       setValidationError("");
  145:     }
  146:   }
  147: 
  148:   function handleSquareClick(row: number, col: number) {
  149:     setSelectedSquare({ row, col });
  150:     if (activeTool === "") {
  151:       // Erase tool active
  152:       const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, row, col, "");
  153:       pushState(nextPlacement);
  154:     } else {
  155:       // Place / Replace with active piece
  156:       const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, row, col, activeTool);
  157:       pushState(nextPlacement);
  158:     }
  159:   }
  160: 
  161:   function handleToolSelect(tool: string) {
  162:     setActiveTool(tool);
  163:     // If a square is already selected and user picks a piece or erase, apply to that square
  164:     if (selectedSquare) {
  165:       const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, selectedSquare.row, selectedSquare.col, tool);
  166:       pushState(nextPlacement);
  167:     }
  168:   }
  169: 
  170:   function handleClearBoard() {
  171:     pushState(EMPTY_PLACEMENT, turn, "-");
  172:     setSelectedSquare(null);
  173:   }
  174: 
  175:   function handleStartingPosition() {
  176:     pushState(START_PLACEMENT, "w", "KQkq");
  177:     setSelectedSquare(null);
  178:   }
  179: 
  180:   function handleFlipBoard() {
  181:     setFlipped((f) => !f);
  182:   }
  183: 
  184:   function handleInvertPieces() {
  185:     const nextPlacement = flipFenPlacement(placement);
  186:     pushState(nextPlacement);
  187:   }
  188: 
  189:   function handleTurnChange(newTurn: "w" | "b") {
  190:     pushState(placement, newTurn, castling);
  191:   }
  192: 
  193:   function handleFenInputChange(value: string) {
  194:     setFenInputText(value);
  195:     const trimmed = value.trim();
  196:     const fields = trimmed.split(/\s+/);
  197:     if (fields.length === 6) {
  198:       const parsed = parseFenPlacement(fields[0]);
  199:       if (parsed) {
  200:         setPlacement(fields[0]);
  201:         setTurn(fields[1] === "b" ? "b" : "w");
  202:         setCastling(fields[2] ?? "-");
  203:         setValidationError("");
  204:         if (trimmed !== history[historyIndex]) {
  205:           const nextHistory = history.slice(0, historyIndex + 1);
  206:           nextHistory.push(trimmed);
  207:           setHistory(nextHistory);
  208:           setHistoryIndex(nextHistory.length - 1);
  209:         }
  210:         return;
  211:       }
  212:     }
  213:     const val = validatePositionFen(trimmed);
  214:     if (!val.isValid) {
  215:       setValidationError(val.error || "Invalid FEN syntax.");
  216:     }
  217:   }
  218: 
  219:   // Keyboard navigation for selected square
  220:   function handleKeyDown(event: React.KeyboardEvent) {
  221:     if (!selectedSquare) return;
  222:     const { row, col } = selectedSquare;
  223:     if (event.key === "Delete" || event.key === "Backspace") {
  224:       event.preventDefault();
  225:       const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, row, col, "");
  226:       pushState(nextPlacement);
  227:       return;
  228:     }
  229:     const key = event.key;
  230:     if (/^[prnbqkPRNBQK]$/.test(key)) {
  231:       event.preventDefault();
  232:       const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, row, col, key);
  233:       pushState(nextPlacement);
  234:       setActiveTool(key);
  235:       return;
  236:     }
  237:     if (event.key === "Escape") {
  238:       setSelectedSquare(null);
  239:     }
  240:   }
  241: 
  242:   function handleConfirmClick() {
  243:     if (validationError) {
  244:       return;
  245:     }
  246:     if (!validation.isValid) {
  247:       setValidationError(validation.error || "Cannot confirm: position is not valid.");
  248:       return;
  249:     }
  250:     onConfirm(currentFen);
  251:   }
  252: 
  253:   const pieceAssetUrl = (piece: string) => {
  254:     const color = piece === piece.toUpperCase() ? "w" : "b";
  255:     const type = piece.toLowerCase();
  256:     return `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}assets/pieces/standalone/${color}${type}.svg`;
  257:   };
  258: 
  259:   return (
  260:     <div className="modal-backdrop" role="presentation" onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}>
  261:       <section
  262:         className="transfer-dialog manual-editor-modal"
  263:         role="dialog"
  264:         aria-modal="true"
  265:         aria-label={title}
  266:       >
  267:         <header>
  268:           <div>
  269:             <h2>{title}</h2>
  270:             {subtitle && <p>{subtitle}</p>}
  271:           </div>
  272:           <button
  273:             type="button"
  274:             aria-label="Close dialog"
  275:             title="Cancel and close"
  276:             onClick={onCancel}
  277:           >
  278:             ×
  279:           </button>
  280:         </header>
  281: 
  282:         {showPresets && (
  283:           <div className="editor-presets-bar">
  284:             <span>START FROM:</span>
  285:             {currentAnalysisFen && (
  286:               <button
  287:                 type="button"
  288:                 className="preset-btn"
  289:                 onClick={() => {
  290:                   const fields = currentAnalysisFen.trim().split(/\s+/);
  291:                   if (fields.length === 6 && parseFenPlacement(fields[0])) {
  292:                     pushState(fields[0], fields[1] === "b" ? "b" : "w", fields[2]);
  293:                   }
  294:                 }}
  295:               >
  296:                 Edit Current Board
  297:               </button>
  298:             )}
  299:             <button type="button" className="preset-btn" onClick={handleClearBoard}>
  300:               Empty Board
  301:             </button>
  302:             <button type="button" className="preset-btn" onClick={handleStartingPosition}>
  303:               Standard Starting Board
  304:             </button>
  305:           </div>
  306:         )}
  307: 
  308:         {cropPreviewUrl && (
  309:           <div className="recognition-layout">
  310:             <img className="crop-preview" src={cropPreviewUrl} alt="Selected chess diagram crop" />
  311:             <div className="recognition-summary" role="status">
  312:               {recognitionStatus === "loading" && (
  313:                 <>
  314:                   <span className="spinner" /> Recognizing pieces…
  315:                 </>
  316:               )}
  317:               {recognitionStatus === "complete" && (
  318:                 <>
  319:                   <strong>Model-recognized position · Editable below</strong>
  320:                   <span>
  321:                     {recognitionResult?.piece_count ?? 0} pieces recognized · confidence{" "}
  322:                     {recognitionResult?.board_confidence?.toFixed(2) ?? "—"}
  323:                   </span>
  324:                   <span>
  325:                     Orientation: {recognitionResult?.orientation ?? "normal"}. Use "Flip position 180°" if needed.
  326:                   </span>
  327:                   {onRetryRecognition && (
  328:                     <button
  329:                       type="button"
  330:                       className="mini-button retry-btn"
  331:                       onClick={onRetryRecognition}
  332:                     >
  333:                       ↻ Retry recognition
  334:                     </button>
  335:                   )}
  336:                 </>
  337:               )}
  338:               {recognitionStatus === "failed" && (
  339:                 <>
  340:                   <strong>Automatic recognition did not complete.</strong>
  341:                   <span>{recognitionError || "Check the diagram crop and set up the pieces manually."}</span>
  342:                   {onRetryRecognition && (
  343:                     <button
  344:                       type="button"
  345:                       className="mini-button retry-btn"
  346:                       onClick={onRetryRecognition}
  347:                     >
  348:                       ↻ Retry recognition
  349:                     </button>
  350:                   )}
  351:                 </>
  352:               )}
  353:             </div>
  354:           </div>
  355:         )}
  356: 
  357:         <div className="manual-editor-workspace">
  358:           {/* Left: The Board */}
  359:           <div className="manual-board-container">
  360:             <div
  361:               className={`manual-chessboard ${flipped ? "flipped" : ""}`}
  362:               role="grid"
  363:               aria-label="Interactive chess position editor"
  364:             >
  365:               {Array.from({ length: 64 }, (_, index) => {
  366:                 const visibleRow = Math.floor(index / 8);
  367:                 const visibleCol = index % 8;

  369:                 const boardCol = flipped ? 7 - visibleCol : visibleCol;
  370:                 const piece = currentBoard[boardRow]?.[boardCol] ?? "";

  373:                 const fileLetter = "abcdefgh"[boardCol];
  374:                 const rankNumber = 8 - boardRow;
  375:                 const squareCode = `${fileLetter}${rankNumber}`;
  376: 
  377:                 return (
  378:                   <button
  379:                     key={`${boardRow}-${boardCol}`}
  380:                     type="button"

  382:                       isSelected ? "selected-square" : ""
  383:                     }`}
  384:                     aria-label={`${squareCode}: ${PIECE_LABELS[piece] || "Empty"}`}
  385:                     title={`${squareCode}: ${PIECE_LABELS[piece] || "Empty square"}`}
  386:                     onClick={() => handleSquareClick(boardRow, boardCol)}
  387:                     draggable={Boolean(piece)}
  388:                     onDragStart={(event) => {
  389:                       event.dataTransfer.setData(
  390:                         "application/json",
  391:                         JSON.stringify({ type: "board-square", fromRow: boardRow, fromCol: boardCol, piece })
  392:                       );
  393:                       setSelectedSquare({ row: boardRow, col: boardCol });
  394:                     }}
  395:                     onDragOver={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
  396:                     onDrop={(event) => {
  397:                       event.preventDefault();
  398:                       try {
  399:                         const raw = event.dataTransfer.getData("application/json");
  400:                         if (!raw) return;
  401:                         const data = JSON.parse(raw) as {
  402:                           type: string;
  403:                           fromRow?: number;
  404:                           fromCol?: number;
  405:                           piece: string;
  406:                         };
  407:                         if (data.type === "board-square" && data.fromRow !== undefined && data.fromCol !== undefined) {
  408:                           if (data.fromRow === boardRow && data.fromCol === boardCol) return;
  409:                           const nextPlacement = moveFenPiece(placement, data.fromRow, data.fromCol, boardRow, boardCol);
  410:                           pushState(nextPlacement);
  411:                           setSelectedSquare({ row: boardRow, col: boardCol });
  412:                         } else if (data.type === "palette-piece" && data.piece) {
  413:                           const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, boardRow, boardCol, data.piece);
  414:                           pushState(nextPlacement);
  415:                           setSelectedSquare({ row: boardRow, col: boardCol });
  416:                         }
  417:                       } catch {
  418:                         // ignore malformed drag payload
  419:                       }
  420:                     }}
  421:                   >
  422:                     {piece && (
  423:                       <img
  424:                         draggable={false}
  425:                         alt={PIECE_LABELS[piece]}
  426:                         src={pieceAssetUrl(piece)}
  427:                       />
  428:                     )}
  429:                     {visibleCol === 0 && (
  430:                       <span className={`rank-coordinate ${isLight ? "coord-dark" : "coord-light"}`}>
  431:                         {rankNumber}
  432:                       </span>
  433:                     )}
  434:                     {visibleRow === 7 && (
  435:                       <span className={`file-coordinate ${isLight ? "coord-dark" : "coord-light"}`}>
  436:                         {fileLetter}
  437:                       </span>
  438:                     )}
  439:                   </button>
  440:                 );
  441:               })}
  442:             </div>
  443:

  449:                 title="Flip board perspective"

  464:                 disabled={historyIndex <= 0}

==================================================
FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\notation-detection.ts
==================================================
    1: import type { Candidate } from "./model";
    2: 
    3: export type NotationHighlightKind = "continuous" | "variation" | "prose";
    4: 
    5: export type NotationHighlight = {
    6:   id: string;
    7:   candidateId: string;
    8:   kind: NotationHighlightKind;
    9:   text: string;
   10:   x: number;
   11:   y: number;
   12:   width: number;
   13:   height: number;
   14: };
   15: 
   16: export type PDFTextItemInput = {
   17:   str: string;
   18:   transform: number[];
   19:   width: number;
   20:   height: number;
   21: };
   22: 
   23: const MOVE_NUMBER_REGEX = /\b\d{1,3}\s*(\.{1,3}|…)/;
   24: const FIGURINE_REGEX = /[♔♕♖♗♘♙♚♛♜♝♞♟]/;
   25: const SAN_MOVE_REGEX = /\b([KQRBN]?[a-h]?[1-8]?x?[a-h][1-8](?:=[QRBN])?[\+#]?|O-O(?:-O)?[\+#]?)\b/;
   26: const FIGURINE_MOVE_REGEX = /[♔♕♖♗♘♙♚♛♜♝♞♟]x?[a-h][1-8](?:=[QRBN])?[\+#]?/;
   27: const CHESS_SYMBOL_REGEX = /([!?]{1,2}|(\+\/?-|-\/?\+|\+\/=|=\/\+|±|∓|⩲|⩱|=|∞|⨁))/;
   28: const CHESS_PROSE_REGEX = /\b(diagram|position|white to|black to|wins|draw|mate in|play|move|variation|defense|attack|advantage)\b/i;
   29: 
   30: export function isChessNotationText(text: string): { isNotation: boolean; kind: NotationHighlightKind } {
   31:   const trimmed = text.trim();
   32:   if (!trimmed || trimmed.length < 2) return { isNotation: false, kind: "continuous" };
   33: 
   34:   const hasMoveNum = MOVE_NUMBER_REGEX.test(trimmed);
   35:   const hasSan = SAN_MOVE_REGEX.test(trimmed);
   36:   const hasFigurine = FIGURINE_REGEX.test(trimmed) || FIGURINE_MOVE_REGEX.test(trimmed);
   37:   const hasSymbol = CHESS_SYMBOL_REGEX.test(trimmed);
   38:   const hasProse = CHESS_PROSE_REGEX.test(trimmed);
   39: 
   40:   if (trimmed.startsWith("(") || (trimmed.includes("(") && (hasSan || hasFigurine))) {
   41:     return { isNotation: true, kind: "variation" };
   42:   }
   43: 
   44:   if (hasMoveNum && (hasSan || hasFigurine)) {
   45:     return { isNotation: true, kind: "continuous" };
   46:   }
   47: 
   48:   if (hasSan && (hasFigurine || hasSymbol || trimmed.split(/\s+/).length >= 2)) {
   49:     return { isNotation: true, kind: "continuous" };
   50:   }
   51: 
   52:   if (hasFigurine && (hasSan || hasSymbol || hasMoveNum)) {
   53:     return { isNotation: true, kind: "continuous" };
   54:   }
   55: 
   56:   if (hasProse && (hasMoveNum || hasSan || hasFigurine || trimmed.toLowerCase().includes("diagram"))) {
   57:     return { isNotation: true, kind: "prose" };
   58:   }
   59: 
   60:   return { isNotation: false, kind: "continuous" };
   61: }
   62: 
   63: type NormalizedTextLine = {
   64:   text: string;
   65:   x: number;
   66:   y: number;
   67:   width: number;
   68:   height: number;
   69:   kind: NotationHighlightKind;
   70: };
   71: 
   72: export function extractNotationHighlights(
   73:   items: PDFTextItemInput[],
   74:   viewport: { width: number; height: number; convertToViewportPoint?: (x: number, y: number) => [number, number] },
   75:   candidates: Candidate[],
   76: ): NotationHighlight[] {
   77:   if (!items.length || !candidates.length || viewport.width <= 0 || viewport.height <= 0) {
   78:     return [];
   79:   }
   80: 
   81:   const textItems: { text: string; x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }[] = [];
   82: 
   83:   for (const item of items) {
   84:     if (!item.str || !item.str.trim()) continue;
   85:     const tx = item.transform[4];
   86:     const ty = item.transform[5];
   87:     const w = item.width || Math.abs(item.transform[0]) || 8;
   88:     const h = item.height || Math.abs(item.transform[3]) || 10;
   89: 
   90:     let x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number;
   91:     if (typeof viewport.convertToViewportPoint === "function") {
   92:       [x1, y1] = viewport.convertToViewportPoint(tx, ty + h);
   93:       [x2, y2] = viewport.convertToViewportPoint(tx + w, ty);
   94:     } else {
   95:       x1 = tx;
   96:       y1 = viewport.height - (ty + h);
   97:       x2 = tx + w;
   98:       y2 = viewport.height - ty;
   99:     }
  100: 
  101:     const normX = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Math.min(x1, x2) / viewport.width));
  102:     const normY = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Math.min(y1, y2) / viewport.height));
  103:     const normW = Math.max(0.005, Math.min(1 - normX, Math.abs(x2 - x1) / viewport.width));
  104:     const normH = Math.max(0.005, Math.min(1 - normY, Math.abs(y2 - y1) / viewport.height));
  105: 
  106:     textItems.push({
  107:       text: item.str,
  108:       x: normX,
  109:       y: normY,
  110:       width: normW,
  111:       height: normH,
  112:     });
  113:   }
  114: 
  115:   if (!textItems.length) return [];
  116: 
  117:   textItems.sort((a, b) => (Math.abs(a.y - b.y) < 0.008 ? a.x - b.x : a.y - b.y));
  118: 
  119:   const lines: NormalizedTextLine[] = [];
  120:   let currentGroup: typeof textItems = [];
  121: 
  122:   function flushGroup() {
  123:     if (!currentGroup.length) return;
  124:     const text = currentGroup.map((i) => i.text).join(" ").trim();
  125:     const { isNotation, kind } = isChessNotationText(text);
  126: 
  127:     if (isNotation) {
  128:       const minX = Math.min(...currentGroup.map((i) => i.x));
  129:       const minY = Math.min(...currentGroup.map((i) => i.y));
  130:       const maxX = Math.max(...currentGroup.map((i) => i.x + i.width));
  131:       const maxY = Math.max(...currentGroup.map((i) => i.y + i.height));
  132:       lines.push({
  133:         text,
  134:         x: Math.max(0, minX - 0.003),
  135:         y: Math.max(0, minY - 0.002),
  136:         width: Math.min(1 - minX, (maxX - minX) + 0.006),
  137:         height: Math.min(1 - minY, (maxY - minY) + 0.004),
  138:         kind,
  139:       });
  140:     }
  141:     currentGroup = [];
  142:   }
  143: 
  144:   for (const item of textItems) {
  145:     if (!currentGroup.length) {
  146:       currentGroup.push(item);
  147:     } else {
  148:       const last = currentGroup[currentGroup.length - 1];
  149:       const sameLine = Math.abs(item.y - last.y) < 0.01;
  150:       const nearbyX = item.x - (last.x + last.width) < 0.08;
  151:       if (sameLine && nearbyX) {
  152:         currentGroup.push(item);
  153:       } else {
  154:         flushGroup();
  155:         currentGroup.push(item);
  156:       }
  157:     }
  158:   }
  159:   flushGroup();
  160: 
  161:   if (!lines.length) return [];
  162: 
  163:   const result: NotationHighlight[] = [];
  164: 
  165:   for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index++) {
  166:     const line = lines[index];
  167:     let bestCandidate = candidates[0];
  168:     let bestDistance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  169: 
  170:     for (const candidate of candidates) {
  171:       const candBottom = candidate.y + candidate.height;
  172:       const candTop = candidate.y;
  173:       const candCenterX = candidate.x + candidate.width / 2;
  174:       const lineCenterX = line.x + line.width / 2;
  175: 
  176:       const isBelow = line.y >= candTop - 0.02 && line.y <= candBottom + 0.35;
  177:       const xDist = Math.abs(lineCenterX - candCenterX);
  178:       const yDist = line.y >= candBottom ? (line.y - candBottom) : Math.abs(line.y - candTop);
  179: 
  180:       const score = (isBelow ? 0 : 10) + yDist * 2 + xDist * 1.5;
  181: 
  182:       if (score < bestDistance) {
  183:         bestDistance = score;
  184:         bestCandidate = candidate;
  185:       }
  186:     }
  187: 
  188:     result.push({
  189:       id: "highlight-" + bestCandidate.id + "-" + (index + 1),
  190:       candidateId: bestCandidate.id,
  191:       kind: line.kind,
  192:       text: line.text,
  193:       x: line.x,
  194:       y: line.y,
  195:       width: line.width,
  196:       height: line.height,
  197:     });
  198:   }
  199: 
  200:   return result;
  201: }

FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\NotationLearner.tsx
==================================================
    1: import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
    2: import { Chess, type Square } from "chess.js";
    3: import { ChevronFirst, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, ChevronLast, RotateCw, BookOpen, GraduationCap } from "lucide-react";
    4: import "./notation-learner.css";
    5: 
    6: const FILES = "abcdefgh";
    7: const PIECES: Record<string, string> = {
    8:   wk:"?", wq:"?", wr:"?", wb:"?", wn:"?", wp:"?",
    9:   bk:"?", bq:"?", br:"?", bb:"?", bn:"?", bp:"?"
   10: };
   11: 
   12: function parseLine(input: string) {
   13:   const chess = new Chess();
   14:   const cleaned = input
   15:     .replace(/\{[^}]*\}/g, " ")
   16:     .replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, " ")
   17:     .replace(/^\s*\[.*\]\s*$/gm, " ")
   18:     .replace(/\d+\.(\.\.)?/g, " ")
   19:     .replace(/\$\d+/g, " ")
   20:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   21:     .trim();
   22: 
   23:   const tokens = cleaned.split(" ").filter(Boolean)
   24:     .filter((x) => !["1-0", "0-1", "1/2-1/2", "*"].includes(x));
   25: 
   26:   const moves: string[] = [];
   27:   for (const token of tokens) {
   28:     try {
   29:       const move = chess.move(token);
   30:       moves.push(move.san);
   31:     } catch {
   32:       throw new Error(`Invalid or illegal move: ${token}`);
   33:     }
   34:   }
   35:   return moves;
   36: }
   37: 
   38: export default function NotationLearner() {
   39:   const [input, setInput] = useState("1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bb5 a6");
   40:   const [moves, setMoves] = useState<string[]>(() => parseLine("1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6 3. Bb5 a6"));
   41:   const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
   42:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   43:   const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState(false);
   44:   const [training, setTraining] = useState(false);
   45:   const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState(false);
   46:   const [selectedSquare, setSelectedSquare] = useState<Square | null>(null);
   47: 
   48: 
   49:   const positions = useMemo(() => {
   50:     const game = new Chess();
   51:     const result = [game.fen()];
   52:     for (const san of moves) {
   53:       game.move(san);
   54:       result.push(game.fen());
   55:     }
   56:     return result;
   57:   }, [moves]);
   58: 
   59:   const game = useMemo(() => new Chess(positions[index]), [positions, index]);
   60:   const legalTargets = useMemo(() => {
   61:     if (!selectedSquare) return [];
   62:     return game.moves({ square: selectedSquare, verbose: true }).map(move => move.to);
   63:   }, [game, selectedSquare]);
   64:   const history = useMemo(() => {
   65:     const replay = new Chess();
   66:     const result = [];
   67:     for (const san of moves.slice(0, index)) result.push(replay.move(san));
   68:     return result;
   69:   }, [moves, index]);
   70: 
   71:   const lastMove = history.at(-1);
   72:   const board = game.board();
   73:   const squares = flipped
   74:     ? [...Array(8)].flatMap((_, r) => [...Array(8)].map((__, f) => `${FILES[7-f]}${r+1}`))
   75:     : [...Array(8)].flatMap((_, r) => [...Array(8)].map((__, f) => `${FILES[f]}${8-r}`));
   76: 
   77:   function loadLine() {
   78:     try {
   79:       const parsed = parseLine(input);
   80:       setMoves(parsed);
   81:       setIndex(0);
   82:       setError("");
   83:       setRevealed(false);
   84:     } catch (cause) {
   85:       setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause));
   86:     }
   87:   }
   88: 
   89:   const piece = lastMove?.piece
   90:     ? ({ p: "Pawn", n: "Knight", b: "Bishop", r: "Rook", q: "Queen", k: "King" } as Record<string,string>)[lastMove.piece]
   91:     : "Starting position";
   92: 
   93:   return (
   94:     <main className="notation-workspace">
   95:       <header className="notation-header">
   96:         <div>
   97:           <span className="notation-eyebrow">CHESSBOOK TRAINING</span>
   98:           <h1>Notation Learner</h1>
   99:           <p>Connect every chess symbol to the position it creates.</p>
  100:         </div>
  101:         <div className="notation-header-actions">
  102:           <button onClick={() => setTraining(v => !v)} className={training ? "nl-active" : ""}>
  103:             <GraduationCap size={16}/> {training ? "Training ON" : "Training mode"}
  104:           </button>
  105:           <button onClick={() => setFlipped(v => !v)}><RotateCw size={16}/> Flip board</button>
  106:         </div>
  107:       </header>
  108: 
  109:       <section className="notation-input-panel">
  110:         <label htmlFor="notation-input">Paste PGN moves or a SAN line</label>
  111:         <textarea id="notation-input" value={input} onChange={e => setInput(e.target.value)} rows={3} spellCheck={false}/>
  112:         <div className="nl-input-footer">
  113:           <span>Example: 1. e4 e5 2. Nf3 Nc6</span>
  114:           <button className="nl-primary" onClick={loadLine}><BookOpen size={15}/> Load notation</button>
  115:         </div>
  116:         {error && <p className="nl-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
  117:       </section>
  118: 
  119:       <div className="notation-layout">
  120:         <section className="nl-board-card">
  121:           <div className="nl-board-meta">
  122:             <span>POSITION {index} / {moves.length}</span>
  123:             <strong>{game.turn() === "w" ? "White to move" : "Black to move"}</strong>
  124:           </div>
  125:           <div className="nl-board" role="grid" aria-label="Interactive chess position">
  126:             {squares.map(name => {
  127:               const square = name as Square;
  128:               const file = FILES.indexOf(name[0]);
  129:               const rank = Number(name[1]);
  130:               const item = board[8-rank][file];
  131:               const isLast = lastMove && (lastMove.from === square || lastMove.to === square);
  132:               const dark = (file + rank) % 2 === 0;
  133:               return (
  134:                 <div key={name} role="gridcell" aria-label={`${name}${item ? ` ${item.color === "w" ? "white" : "black"} ${item.type}` : ""}`}
  135:                   className={`nl-square ${dark ? "dark" : "light"} ${isLast ? "last" : ""} ${selectedSquare === square ? "nl-selected" : ""} ${legalTargets.includes(square) ? "nl-legal-target" : ""}`}
  136:                   onClick={() => setSelectedSquare(current => current === square ? null : square)}
  137:                   onKeyDown={event => {
  138:                     if (event.key === "Enter" || event.key === " ") {
  139:                       event.preventDefault();
  140:                       setSelectedSquare(current => current === square ? null : square);
  141:                     }
  142:                   }}
  143:                   tabIndex={0}
  144:                   aria-selected={selectedSquare === square}
  145:                   title={`${square}${item ? ` � ${item.color === "w" ? "White" : "Black"} ${item.type}` : ""}`}>
  146:                   {item && <span className={`nl-piece ${item.color === "w" ? "white-piece" : "black-piece"}`}>{PIECES[item.color + item.type]}</span>}
  147:                   {file === (flipped ? 7 : 0) && <small className="nl-rank">{rank}</small>}
  148:                   {rank === (flipped ? 1 : 1) && <small className="nl-file">{name[0]}</small>}
  149:                 </div>
  150:               );
  151:             })}
  152:           </div>
  153:           {selectedSquare && (
  154:             <div className="nl-square-inspector" aria-live="polite">
  155:               <strong>{selectedSquare}</strong>
  156:               <span>
  157:                 {game.get(selectedSquare)
  158:                   ? `${game.get(selectedSquare)?.color === "w" ? "White" : "Black"} ${({
  159:                       p: "Pawn", n: "Knight", b: "Bishop",
  160:                       r: "Rook", q: "Queen", k: "King"
  161:                     } as Record<string, string>)[game.get(selectedSquare)!.type]}`
  162:                   : "Empty square"}
  163:               </span>
  164:               <span>
  165:                 {legalTargets.length
  166:                   ? `${legalTargets.length} legal destination${legalTargets.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} highlighted`
  167:                   : "No legal moves from this square"}
  168:               </span>
  169:             </div>
  170:           )}
  171:           <div className="nl-controls">
  172:             <button aria-label="First move" onClick={() => {setIndex(0);setRevealed(false)}}><ChevronFirst/></button>
  173:             <button aria-label="Previous move" onClick={() => {setIndex(v => Math.max(0,v-1));setRevealed(false)}}><ChevronLeft/></button>
  174:             <span>{index === 0 ? "Starting position" : `${Math.ceil(index/2)}${index%2 ? "." : "..."}`}</span>
  175:             <button aria-label="Next move" disabled={index >= moves.length || (training && !revealed)} onClick={() => {setIndex(v => Math.min(moves.length,v+1));setRevealed(false)}}><ChevronRight/></button>
  176:             <button aria-label="Last move" disabled={training} onClick={() => setIndex(moves.length)}><ChevronLast/></button>
  177:           </div>
  178:         </section>
  179: 
  180:         <aside className="nl-notation-card">
  181:           <div className="nl-card-title"><strong>GAME NOTATION</strong><span>{moves.length} plies</span></div>
  182:           <div className="nl-move-list">
  183:             {moves.length === 0 && <p className="nl-muted">Load a move sequence to begin.</p>}
  184:             {Array.from({length: Math.ceil(moves.length/2)}, (_, row) => (
  185:               <div className="nl-move-row" key={row}>
  186:                 <span>{row+1}.</span>
  187:                 {[0,1].map(side => {
  188:                   const ply = row*2+side;
  189:                   if (!moves[ply]) return <i key={side}/>;
  190:                   const hidden = training && ply === index && !revealed;
  191:                   return <button key={side} className={index === ply+1 ? "selected" : ""}
  192:                     onClick={() => {setIndex(ply+1);setRevealed(false)}}
  193:                     disabled={hidden}>
  194:                     {hidden ? "?" : moves[ply]}
  195:                   </button>;
  196:                 })}
  197:               </div>
  198:             ))}
  199:           </div>
  200:           <div className="nl-learning">
  201:             <span className="notation-eyebrow">MOVE EXPLANATION</span>
  202:             {index === 0
  203:               ? <p>Start from the initial position. Select a move to see how the notation changes the board.</p>
  204:               : <><strong>{piece}</strong><p>{lastMove?.color === "w" ? "White" : "Black"} moved from <b>{lastMove?.from}</b> to <b>{lastMove?.to}</b>.</p><p className="nl-san">{lastMove?.san}</p></>}
  205:             {training && index < moves.length && !revealed && <button className="nl-primary" onClick={() => setRevealed(true)}>Reveal next move: {moves[index]}</button>}
  206:           </div>
  207:         </aside>
  208:       </div>
  209:       <p className="nl-fen"><strong>FEN</strong> {game.fen()}</p>
  210:     </main>
  211:   );
  212: }
  213: 
  214:

FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\opening-explorer-api.ts
==================================================
    1: export type OpeningExplorerMove = {
    2:   uci: string;
    3:   san: string;
    4:   white: number;
    5:   draws: number;
    6:   black: number;
    7:   opening?: { eco?: string; name: string } | null;
    8: };
    9: 
   10: export type OpeningExplorerStats = {
   11:   white: number;
   12:   draws: number;
   13:   black: number;
   14:   total: number;
   15:   moves: OpeningExplorerMove[];
   16:   opening?: { eco?: string; name: string } | null;
   17: };
   18: 
   19: function count(value: unknown): number {
   20:   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0
   21:     ? value
   22:     : 0;
   23: }
   24: 
   25: function opening(value: unknown): OpeningExplorerStats["opening"] {
   26:   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
   27:   const item = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   28:   if (typeof item.name !== "string") return null;
   29:   return {
   30:     name: item.name,
   31:     ...(typeof item.eco === "string" ? { eco: item.eco } : {}),
   32:   };
   33: }
   34: 
   35: export async function fetchOpeningExplorerStats(
   36:   uciMoves: string[],
   37:   signal?: AbortSignal,
   38: ): Promise<OpeningExplorerStats> {
   39:   const query = new URLSearchParams();
   40:   query.set("variant", "standard");
   41:   query.set("moves", "8");
   42:   if (uciMoves.length) query.set("play", uciMoves.join(","));
   43: 
   44:   const response = await fetch(
   45:     `/api/lichess/opening-explorer?${query.toString()}`,
   46:     {
   47:       method: "GET",
   48:       credentials: "same-origin",
   49:       headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
   50:       signal,
   51:     },
   52:   );
   53: 
   54:   if (!response.ok) {
   55:     throw new Error(`Opening Explorer request failed (${response.status}).`);
   56:   }
   57: 
   58:   const raw: unknown = await response.json();
   59:   if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") {
   60:     throw new Error("Opening Explorer returned an invalid response.");
   61:   }
   62: 
   63:   const data = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
   64:   const white = count(data.white);
   65:   const draws = count(data.draws);
   66:   const black = count(data.black);
   67: 
   68:   const moves: OpeningExplorerMove[] = Array.isArray(data.moves)
   69:     ? data.moves.flatMap((entry): OpeningExplorerMove[] => {
   70:         if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") return [];
   71:         const item = entry as Record<string, unknown>;
   72:         if (typeof item.uci !== "string" || typeof item.san !== "string") return [];
   73:         return [{
   74:           uci: item.uci,
   75:           san: item.san,
   76:           white: count(item.white),
   77:           draws: count(item.draws),
   78:           black: count(item.black),
   79:           opening: opening(item.opening),
   80:         }];
   81:       })
   82:     : [];
   83: 
   84:   return {
   85:     white,
   86:     draws,
   87:     black,
   88:     total: white + draws + black,
   89:     moves,
   90:     opening: opening(data.opening),
   91:   };
   92: }
   93: 
   94: export type LichessConnectionStatus = {
   95:   connected: boolean;
   96:   configured: boolean;
   97: };
   98: 
   99: export async function getLichessConnectionStatus(
  100:   signal?: AbortSignal,
  101: ): Promise<LichessConnectionStatus> {
  102:   const response = await fetch("/api/lichess/status", {
  103:     method: "GET",
  104:     credentials: "same-origin",
  105:     headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
  106:     cache: "no-store",
  107:     signal,
  108:   });
  109:   if (!response.ok) {
  110:     throw new Error(`Connection status request failed (${response.status}).`);
  111:   }
  112:   const value: unknown = await response.json();
  113:   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") {
  114:     throw new Error("Invalid connection status response.");
  115:   }
  116:   const status = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  117:   return {
  118:     connected: status.connected === true,
  119:     configured: status.configured === true,
  120:   };
  121: }
  122: 
  123: export async function disconnectLichess(): Promise<void> {
  124:   const response = await fetch("/api/lichess/disconnect", {
  125:     method: "POST",
  126:     credentials: "same-origin",
  127:     headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
  128:   });
  129:   if (!response.ok) {
  130:     throw new Error(`Disconnect request failed (${response.status}).`);
  131:   }
  132: }

FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\opening-explorer.ts
==================================================
    1: import { Chess, type Square } from "chess.js";
    2: 
    3: export type OpeningLineResult = {
    4:   fen: string;
    5:   sanMoves: string[];
    6:   uciMoves: string[];
    7:   error: string | null;
    8: };
    9: 
   10: export function parseOpeningLine(input: string): OpeningLineResult {
   11:   const chess = new Chess();
   12:   const sanMoves: string[] = [];
   13:   const uciMoves: string[] = [];
   14: 
   15:   const tokens = input
   16:     .replace(/\{[^}]*\}/g, " ")
   17:     .replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, " ")
   18:     .replace(/\d+\.(\.\.)?/g, " ")
   19:     .replace(/\$\d+/g, " ")
   20:     .split(/\s+/)
   21:     .filter(Boolean)
   22:     .filter((token) => !["1-0", "0-1", "1/2-1/2", "*"].includes(token));
   23: 
   24:   for (const token of tokens) {
   25:     try {
   26:       const uci = token.match(/^([a-h][1-8])([a-h][1-8])([qrbn])?$/i);
   27:       const move = uci
   28:         ? chess.move({
   29:             from: uci[1].toLowerCase() as Square,
   30:             to: uci[2].toLowerCase() as Square,
   31:             promotion: uci[3]?.toLowerCase(),
   32:           })
   33:         : chess.move(token);
   34: 
   35:       sanMoves.push(move.san);
   36:       uciMoves.push(`${move.from}${move.to}${move.promotion ?? ""}`);
   37:     } catch {
   38:       return {
   39:         fen: chess.fen(),
   40:         sanMoves,
   41:         uciMoves,
   42:         error: `Invalid or illegal move: ${token}`,
   43:       };
   44:     }
   45:   }
   46: 
   47:   return { fen: chess.fen(), sanMoves, uciMoves, error: null };
   48: }
   49: 
   50: export function openingPositionAt(input: string, ply: number): OpeningLineResult {
   51:   const parsed = parseOpeningLine(input);
   52:   if (parsed.error) return parsed;
   53: 
   54:   const limit = Math.max(0, Math.min(parsed.sanMoves.length, Math.floor(ply)));
   55:   const chess = new Chess();
   56:   const sanMoves: string[] = [];
   57:   const uciMoves: string[] = [];
   58: 
   59:   for (let i = 0; i < limit; i++) {
   60:     const move = chess.move(parsed.sanMoves[i]);
   61:     sanMoves.push(move.san);
   62:     uciMoves.push(`${move.from}${move.to}${move.promotion ?? ""}`);
   63:   }
   64: 
   65:   return { fen: chess.fen(), sanMoves, uciMoves, error: null };
   66: }

    1: import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

   38: 
   39: *`)).toThrow(/no moves/i);

FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\pgn-import.ts
==================================================
    1: import { Chess } from "chess.js";
    2: import { START_FEN } from "./model";
    3: 
    4: export type ImportedPgn = {
    5:   headers: Record<string, string>;
    6:   lineBaseFen: string;
    7:   moves: string[];
    8:   finalFen: string;
    9: };
   10: 
   11: export function parsePgn(pgn: string): ImportedPgn {
   12:   const source = pgn.trim();
   13: 
   14:   if (!source) {
   15:     throw new Error("Paste or select a PGN game first.");
   16:   }
   17: 
   18:   const parsed = new Chess();
   19: 
   20:   try {
   21:     parsed.loadPgn(source);
   22:   } catch (cause) {
   23:     const detail = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Unknown PGN error";
   24:     throw new Error(`Invalid PGN: ${detail}`);
   25:   }
   26: 
   27:   const headers = parsed.getHeaders();
   28:   const customFen = headers.FEN?.trim();
   29:   const lineBaseFen = customFen || START_FEN;
   30: 
   31:   let replay: Chess;
   32: 
   33:   try {
   34:     replay = new Chess(lineBaseFen);
   35:   } catch {
   36:     throw new Error("The PGN contains an invalid starting FEN.");
   37:   }
   38: 
   39:   const moves = parsed.history();
   40: 
   41:   if (moves.length === 0) {
   42:     throw new Error("The PGN contains no moves.");
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   try {
   46:     for (const san of moves) {
   47:       replay.move(san);
   48:     }
   49:   } catch (cause) {
   50:     const detail = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Unknown move error";
   51:     throw new Error(`Could not replay the imported game: ${detail}`);
   52:   }
   53: 
   54:   if (replay.fen() !== parsed.fen()) {
   55:     throw new Error("The imported PGN could not be reproduced from its starting position.");
   56:   }
   57: 
   58:   return {
   59:     headers,
   60:     lineBaseFen,
   61:     moves,
   62:     finalFen: replay.fen(),
   63:   };
   64: }

    1: import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

    3:   buildFullFen,
    4:   countKings,
    5:   EMPTY_PLACEMENT,
    6:   flipFenPlacement,
    7:   moveFenPiece,

   13:   withFenPlacement,
   14: } from "./position-editor";
   15: 
   16: describe("position editor", () => {
   17:   it("parses compressed ranks into all 64 cells", () => {
   18:     const board = parseFenPlacement(START_PLACEMENT);
   19:     expect(board).toHaveLength(8);
   20:     expect(board?.[0]).toEqual(["r", "n", "b", "q", "k", "b", "n", "r"]);
   21:     expect(board?.[3]).toEqual(Array(8).fill(""));
   22:   });
   23: 
   24:   it("adds and removes pieces without changing other squares", () => {
   25:     expect(setFenPiece(EMPTY_PLACEMENT, 4, 3, "Q")).toBe("8/8/8/8/3Q4/8/8/8");
   26:     expect(setFenPiece("8/8/8/8/3Q4/8/8/8", 4, 3, "")).toBe(EMPTY_PLACEMENT);
   27:   });
   28: 
   29:   it("replaces a white piece with a black piece", () => {
   30:     const withWhiteQueen = setFenPiece(EMPTY_PLACEMENT, 4, 3, "Q");
   31:     const withBlackQueen = setFenPiece(withWhiteQueen, 4, 3, "q");
   32:     expect(withBlackQueen).toBe("8/8/8/8/3q4/8/8/8");
   33:   });
   34: 
   35:   it("moves pieces from one square to another", () => {
   36:     const initial = setFenPiece(EMPTY_PLACEMENT, 4, 3, "N");
   37:     const moved = moveFenPiece(initial, 4, 3, 2, 4);
   38:     expect(moved).toBe("8/8/4N3/8/8/8/8/8");
   39:   });
   40: 
   41:   it("rotates all squares by 180 degrees and retains the rest of a FEN", () => {
   42:     expect(flipFenPlacement(START_PLACEMENT)).toBe("RNBKQBNR/PPPPPPPP/8/8/8/8/pppppppp/rnbkqbnr");
   43:     expect(withFenPlacement(`${START_PLACEMENT} b - - 0 1`, EMPTY_PLACEMENT))
   44:       .toBe(`${EMPTY_PLACEMENT} b - - 0 1`);
   45:   });
   46: 
   47:   it("counts white and black kings accurately", () => {
   48:     expect(countKings(EMPTY_PLACEMENT)).toEqual({ whiteKings: 0, blackKings: 0 });
   49:     expect(countKings(START_PLACEMENT)).toEqual({ whiteKings: 1, blackKings: 1 });
   50:     expect(countKings("8/8/8/8/4K3/8/8/4K3")).toEqual({ whiteKings: 2, blackKings: 0 });
   51:   });
   52: 
   53:   it("sanitizes castling rights based on king and rook presence", () => {
   54:     const boardStart = parseFenPlacement(START_PLACEMENT)!;
   55:     expect(sanitizeCastlingRights(boardStart, "KQkq")).toBe("KQkq");
   56: 
   57:     // Remove white king from e1
   58:     boardStart[7][4] = "";
   59:     expect(sanitizeCastlingRights(boardStart, "KQkq")).toBe("kq");
   60: 
   61:     // Remove black kingside rook from h8
   62:     boardStart[0][7] = "";
   63:     expect(sanitizeCastlingRights(boardStart, "kq")).toBe("q");
   64: 
   65:     // Empty board
   66:     const emptyBoard = parseFenPlacement(EMPTY_PLACEMENT)!;
   67:     expect(sanitizeCastlingRights(emptyBoard, "KQkq")).toBe("-");
   68:   });
   69: 
   70:   it("builds a full six-field FEN with appropriate defaults", () => {
   71:     expect(buildFullFen(START_PLACEMENT, "w")).toBe(`${START_PLACEMENT} w KQkq - 0 1`);
   72:     expect(buildFullFen("4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/4K3", "b")).toBe("4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/4K3 b - - 0 1");
   73:   });
   74: 
   75:   it("validates chess positions thoroughly", () => {
   76:     // Valid standard start
   77:     expect(validatePositionFen(`${START_PLACEMENT} w KQkq - 0 1`)).toEqual({ isValid: true });
   78: 
   79:     // Valid custom endgame
   80:     expect(validatePositionFen("4k3/8/8/8/8/8/4P3/4K3 w - - 0 1")).toEqual({ isValid: true });
   81: 
   82:     // Missing white king
   83:     const noWhiteKing = validatePositionFen("4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/8 w - - 0 1");
   84:     expect(noWhiteKing.isValid).toBe(false);
   85:     expect(noWhiteKing.error).toContain("White king");
   86: 
   87:     // Missing black king
   88:     const noBlackKing = validatePositionFen("8/8/8/8/8/8/8/4K3 w - - 0 1");
   89:     expect(noBlackKing.isValid).toBe(false);
   90:     expect(noBlackKing.error).toContain("Black king");
   91: 
   92:     // Multiple kings
   93:     const twoKings = validatePositionFen("4k3/8/8/8/8/8/4K3/4K3 w - - 0 1");
   94:     expect(twoKings.isValid).toBe(false);
   95:     expect(twoKings.error).toContain("White kings");
   96: 
   97:     // Pawns on edge rank
   98:     const edgePawn = validatePositionFen("4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/4KP2 w - - 0 1");
   99:     expect(edgePawn.isValid).toBe(false);
  100:     expect(edgePawn.error).toContain("first or eighth rank");
  101: 
  102:     // Malformed FEN
  103:     expect(validatePositionFen("not-a-fen").isValid).toBe(false);
  104:     expect(validatePositionFen("").isValid).toBe(false);
  105:   });
  106: 
  107:   it("rejects malformed placements", () => {
  108:     expect(parseFenPlacement("9/8/8/8/8/8/8/8")).toBeNull();
  109:     expect(parseFenPlacement("8/8/8/8/8/8/8")).toBeNull();
  110:   });
  111: });


==================================================
FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\position-editor.ts
==================================================
    1: import { Chess, validateFen } from "chess.js";
    2: 
    3: const PIECE_CODES = /^[prnbqkPRNBQK]$/;
    4: 
    5: export const START_PLACEMENT = "rnbqkbnr/pppppppp/8/8/8/8/PPPPPPPP/RNBQKBNR";
    6: export const EMPTY_PLACEMENT = "8/8/8/8/8/8/8/8";
    7: 
    8: export const PIECE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
    9:   "": "Empty square",
   10:   P: "White pawn",
   11:   N: "White knight",
   12:   B: "White bishop",
   13:   R: "White rook",
   14:   Q: "White queen",
   15:   K: "White king",
   16:   p: "Black pawn",
   17:   n: "Black knight",
   18:   b: "Black bishop",
   19:   r: "Black rook",
   20:   q: "Black queen",
   21:   k: "Black king",
   22: };
   23: 
   24: export function parseFenPlacement(placement: string): string[][] | null {
   25:   const ranks = placement.split("/");
   26:   if (ranks.length !== 8) return null;
   27:   const board: string[][] = [];
   28:   for (const rank of ranks) {
   29:     const squares: string[] = [];
   30:     for (const token of rank) {
   31:       if (/^[1-8]$/.test(token)) squares.push(...Array(Number(token)).fill(""));
   32:       else if (PIECE_CODES.test(token)) squares.push(token);
   33:       else return null;
   34:     }
   35:     if (squares.length !== 8) return null;
   36:     board.push(squares);
   37:   }
   38:   return board;
   39: }
   40: 
   41: export function serializeFenPlacement(board: string[][]): string {
   42:   if (board.length !== 8 || board.some((rank) => rank.length !== 8)) {
   43:     throw new Error("A chess position must contain exactly 64 squares.");
   44:   }
   45:   return board.map((rank) => {
   46:     let result = "";
   47:     let empty = 0;
   48:     for (const piece of rank) {
   49:       if (!piece) {
   50:         empty += 1;
   51:       } else {
   52:         if (!PIECE_CODES.test(piece)) throw new Error(`Invalid chess piece code: ${piece}`);
   53:         if (empty) result += empty;
   54:         empty = 0;
   55:         result += piece;
   56:       }
   57:     }
   58:     if (empty) result += empty;
   59:     return result;
   60:   }).join("/");
   61: }
   62: 
   63: export function setFenPiece(placement: string, row: number, col: number, piece: string): string {
   64:   const board = parseFenPlacement(placement);
   65:   if (!board || row < 0 || row > 7 || col < 0 || col > 7) {
   66:     throw new Error("Cannot edit an invalid chessboard position.");
   67:   }
   68:   if (piece && !PIECE_CODES.test(piece)) throw new Error(`Invalid chess piece code: ${piece}`);
   69:   board[row][col] = piece;
   70:   return serializeFenPlacement(board);
   71: }
   72: 
   73: export function moveFenPiece(placement: string, fromRow: number, fromCol: number, toRow: number, toCol: number): string {
   74:   const board = parseFenPlacement(placement);
   75:   if (!board || fromRow < 0 || fromRow > 7 || fromCol < 0 || fromCol > 7 || toRow < 0 || toRow > 7 || toCol < 0 || toCol > 7) {
   76:     throw new Error("Cannot move a piece on an invalid chessboard position.");
   77:   }
   78:   const piece = board[fromRow][fromCol];
   79:   if (!piece) return placement;
   80:   board[fromRow][fromCol] = "";
   81:   board[toRow][toCol] = piece;
   82:   return serializeFenPlacement(board);
   83: }
   84: 
   85: export function flipFenPlacement(placement: string): string {
   86:   const board = parseFenPlacement(placement);
   87:   if (!board) throw new Error("Cannot flip an invalid chessboard position.");
   88:   return serializeFenPlacement(board.reverse().map((rank) => rank.reverse()));
   89: }
   90: 
   91: export function withFenPlacement(fen: string, placement: string): string {
   92:   const fields = fen.trim().split(/\s+/);
   93:   if (fields.length !== 6 || !parseFenPlacement(placement)) {
   94:     throw new Error("A complete six-field FEN is required.");
   95:   }
   96:   fields[0] = placement;
   97:   return fields.join(" ");
   98: }
   99: 
  100: export function countKings(placementOrFen: string): { whiteKings: number; blackKings: number } {
  101:   const placement = placementOrFen.trim().split(/\s+/)[0];
  102:   const board = parseFenPlacement(placement);
  103:   let whiteKings = 0;
  104:   let blackKings = 0;
  105:   if (board) {
  106:     for (const rank of board) {
  107:       for (const square of rank) {
  108:         if (square === "K") whiteKings += 1;
  109:         if (square === "k") blackKings += 1;
  110:       }
  111:     }
  112:   }
  113:   return { whiteKings, blackKings };
  114: }
  115: 
  116: export function sanitizeCastlingRights(board: string[][], currentCastling: string): string {
  117:   if (!currentCastling || currentCastling === "-") return "-";
  118:   let result = "";
  119:   // White king must be on e1 (row 7, col 4)
  120:   const whiteKingOnE1 = board[7]?.[4] === "K";
  121:   if (whiteKingOnE1) {
  122:     if (currentCastling.includes("K") && board[7]?.[7] === "R") result += "K";
  123:     if (currentCastling.includes("Q") && board[7]?.[0] === "R") result += "Q";
  124:   }
  125:   // Black king must be on e8 (row 0, col 4)
  126:   const blackKingOnE8 = board[0]?.[4] === "k";
  127:   if (blackKingOnE8) {
  128:     if (currentCastling.includes("k") && board[0]?.[7] === "r") result += "k";
  129:     if (currentCastling.includes("q") && board[0]?.[0] === "r") result += "q";
  130:   }
  131:   return result || "-";
  132: }
  133: 
  134: export function buildFullFen(
  135:   placement: string,
  136:   turn: "w" | "b" = "w",
  137:   castling?: string,
  138:   enPassant: string = "-",
  139:   halfmove: number = 0,
  140:   fullmove: number = 1
  141: ): string {
  142:   const board = parseFenPlacement(placement);
  143:   if (!board) throw new Error("Invalid piece placement for FEN generation.");
  144:   let effectiveCastling = castling;
  145:   if (!effectiveCastling) {
  146:     effectiveCastling = placement === START_PLACEMENT ? "KQkq" : "-";
  147:   } else {
  148:     effectiveCastling = sanitizeCastlingRights(board, effectiveCastling);
  149:   }
  150:   return `${placement} ${turn} ${effectiveCastling} ${enPassant} ${halfmove} ${fullmove}`;
  151: }
  152: 
  153: export function validatePositionFen(fen: string): { isValid: boolean; error?: string } {
  154:   const trimmed = fen.trim();
  155:   if (!trimmed) {
  156:     return { isValid: false, error: "Position FEN cannot be empty." };
  157:   }
  158:   const fields = trimmed.split(/\s+/);
  159:   if (fields.length !== 6) {
  160:     return { isValid: false, error: "FEN must contain exactly 6 space-separated fields." };
  161:   }
  162:   const [placement, turn] = fields;
  163:   const board = parseFenPlacement(placement);
  164:   if (!board) {
  165:     return { isValid: false, error: "Invalid board piece placement." };
  166:   }
  167: 
  168:   let whiteKings = 0;
  169:   let blackKings = 0;
  170:   let invalidPawns = 0;
  171:   for (let r = 0; r < 8; r++) {
  172:     for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
  173:       const piece = board[r][c];
  174:       if (piece === "K") whiteKings += 1;
  175:       if (piece === "k") blackKings += 1;
  176:       if ((r === 0 || r === 7) && (piece === "P" || piece === "p")) {
  177:         invalidPawns += 1;
  178:       }
  179:     }
  180:   }
  181: 
  182:   if (whiteKings === 0) {
  183:     return { isValid: false, error: "Position is missing a White king (K)." };
  184:   }
  185:   if (whiteKings > 1) {
  186:     return { isValid: false, error: `Position has ${whiteKings} White kings. Exactly one is required.` };
  187:   }
  188:   if (blackKings === 0) {
  189:     return { isValid: false, error: "Position is missing a Black king (k)." };
  190:   }
  191:   if (blackKings > 1) {
  192:     return { isValid: false, error: `Position has ${blackKings} Black kings. Exactly one is required.` };
  193:   }
  194:   if (invalidPawns > 0) {
  195:     return { isValid: false, error: "Pawns cannot be placed on the first or eighth rank." };
  196:   }
  197:   if (turn !== "w" && turn !== "b") {
  198:     return { isValid: false, error: "Side to move must be 'w' (White) or 'b' (Black)." };
  199:   }
  200: 
  201:   const result = validateFen(trimmed);
  202:   if (!result.ok) {
  203:     return { isValid: false, error: result.error || "Invalid chess position." };
  204:   }
  205: 
  206:   try {
  207:     new Chess(trimmed);
  208:   } catch (err: unknown) {
  209:     return { isValid: false, error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) };
  210:   }
  211: 
  212:   return { isValid: true };
  213: }

FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\review-analysis.ts
==================================================
    1: import type { EngineScore } from "./stockfish";
    2: import { ReviewEngine, type ReviewProgress } from "./review-engine";
    3: import type { ReviewGame } from "./review-pgn";
    4: 
    5: export type MoveClassification =
    6:   | "Best"
    7:   | "Excellent"
    8:   | "Good"
    9:   | "Inaccuracy"
   10:   | "Mistake"
   11:   | "Blunder";
   12: 
   13: export type ClassificationThresholds = {
   14:   best: number;
   15:   excellent: number;
   16:   good: number;
   17:   inaccuracy: number;
   18:   mistake: number;
   19: };
   20: 
   21: export const DEFAULT_CLASSIFICATION_THRESHOLDS: ClassificationThresholds = {
   22:   best: 10,
   23:   excellent: 30,
   24:   good: 80,
   25:   inaccuracy: 150,
   26:   mistake: 300,
   27: };
   28: 
   29: export type ReviewedMove = {
   30:   ply: number;
   31:   moveNumber: number;
   32:   color: "w" | "b";
   33:   san: string;
   34:   from: string;
   35:   to: string;
   36:   promotion?: string;
   37:   playedMoveUci: string;
   38:   bestMove?: string;
   39:   playedMoveIsBest: boolean;
   40:   before: EngineScore;
   41:   after: EngineScore;
   42:   evaluationLossCp: number;
   43:   classification: MoveClassification;
   44: };
   45: 
   46: export type ReviewAnalysis = {
   47:   depth: number;
   48:   positionsAnalyzed: number;
   49:   moves: ReviewedMove[];
   50:   positionScores: EngineScore[];
   51: };
   52: 
   53: export type ReviewEnginePort = Pick<ReviewEngine, "analyzePositions">;
   54: 
   55: export function scoreToWhiteCentipawns(score: EngineScore): number {
   56:   if (score.kind === "cp") return score.value;
   57: 
   58:   const distance = Math.min(99999, Math.abs(score.value));
   59:   return Math.sign(score.value || 1) * (100000 - distance);
   60: }
   61: 
   62: export function calculateMoveLoss(
   63:   before: EngineScore,
   64:   after: EngineScore,
   65:   color: "w" | "b",
   66: ): number {
   67:   const beforeCp = scoreToWhiteCentipawns(before);
   68:   const afterCp = scoreToWhiteCentipawns(after);
   69: 
   70:   const loss = color === "w"
   71:     ? beforeCp - afterCp
   72:     : afterCp - beforeCp;
   73: 
   74:   return Math.max(0, Math.round(loss));
   75: }
   76: 
   77: export function classifyMove(
   78:   lossCp: number,
   79:   thresholds: ClassificationThresholds = DEFAULT_CLASSIFICATION_THRESHOLDS,
   80: ): MoveClassification {
   81:   if (!Number.isFinite(lossCp) || lossCp < 0) {
   82:     throw new Error("Evaluation loss must be a finite, non-negative number.");
   83:   }
   84: 
   85:   if (
   86:     thresholds.best < 0 ||
   87:     thresholds.excellent < thresholds.best ||
   88:     thresholds.good < thresholds.excellent ||
   89:     thresholds.inaccuracy < thresholds.good ||
   90:     thresholds.mistake < thresholds.inaccuracy
   91:   ) {
   92:     throw new Error("Classification thresholds must be non-negative and increasing.");
   93:   }
   94: 
   95:   if (lossCp <= thresholds.best) return "Best";
   96:   if (lossCp <= thresholds.excellent) return "Excellent";
   97:   if (lossCp <= thresholds.good) return "Good";
   98:   if (lossCp <= thresholds.inaccuracy) return "Inaccuracy";
   99:   if (lossCp <= thresholds.mistake) return "Mistake";
  100:   return "Blunder";
  101: }
  102: 
  103: export function buildReviewAnalysis(
  104:   review: ReviewGame,
  105:   positionScores: EngineScore[],
  106:   depth: number,
  107:   thresholds: ClassificationThresholds = DEFAULT_CLASSIFICATION_THRESHOLDS,
  108: ): ReviewAnalysis {
  109:   if (positionScores.length !== review.positions.length + 1) {
  110:     throw new Error("Expected one engine evaluation for every game position.");
  111:   }
  112: 
  113:   const moves = review.positions.map((move, index): ReviewedMove => {
  114:     const before = positionScores[index];
  115:     const after = positionScores[index + 1];
  116: 
  117:     if (!before || !after) {
  118:       throw new Error(`Missing engine evaluation around ply ${move.ply}.`);
  119:     }
  120: 
  121:     const playedMoveUci =
  122:       `${move.from}${move.to}${move.promotion ?? ""}`;
  123: 
  124:     const playedMoveIsBest = before.bestMove
  125:       ? before.bestMove.toLowerCase() === playedMoveUci.toLowerCase()
  126:       : false;
  127: 
  128:     const evaluationLossCp = calculateMoveLoss(
  129:       before,
  130:       after,
  131:       move.color,
  132:     );
  133: 
  134:     return {
  135:       ply: move.ply,
  136:       moveNumber: move.moveNumber,
  137:       color: move.color,
  138:       san: move.san,
  139:       from: move.from,
  140:       to: move.to,
  141:       ...(move.promotion ? { promotion: move.promotion } : {}),
  142:       playedMoveUci,
  143:       ...(before.bestMove ? { bestMove: before.bestMove } : {}),
  144:       playedMoveIsBest,
  145:       before,
  146:       after,
  147:       evaluationLossCp,
  148:       classification: playedMoveIsBest
  149:         ? "Best"
  150:         : classifyMove(evaluationLossCp, thresholds),
  151:     };
  152:   });
  153: 
  154:   return {
  155:     depth,
  156:     positionsAnalyzed: positionScores.length,
  157:     moves,
  158:     positionScores,
  159:   };
  160: }
  161: 
  162: export async function analyzeReviewPositions(
  163:   review: ReviewGame,
  164:   engine: ReviewEnginePort,
  165:   options: {
  166:     depth: number;
  167:     onProgress?: (progress: ReviewProgress) => void;
  168:     thresholds?: ClassificationThresholds;
  169:   },
  170: ): Promise<ReviewAnalysis> {
  171:   const depth = Math.max(1, Math.min(24, Math.floor(options.depth)));
  172: 
  173:   const fens = [
  174:     review.startingFen,
  175:     ...review.positions.map((position) => position.afterFen),
  176:   ];
  177: 
  178:   const positionScores = await engine.analyzePositions(fens, {
  179:     depth,
  180:     onProgress: options.onProgress,
  181:   });
  182: 
  183:   return buildReviewAnalysis(
  184:     review,
  185:     positionScores,
  186:     depth,
  187:     options.thresholds,
  188:   );
  189: }

   16:   }

FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\review-engine.ts
==================================================
    1: import { parseUciInfo, type EngineScore } from "./stockfish";
    2: 
    3: export type ReviewProgress = {
    4:   completed: number;
    5:   total: number;
    6:   currentPly: number;
    7: };
    8: 
    9: export type ReviewAnalysisOptions = {
   10:   depth: number;
   11:   onProgress?: (progress: ReviewProgress) => void;
   12: };
   13: 
   14: type ReviewBatch = {
   15:   fens: string[];
   16:   depth: number;
   17:   index: number;
   18:   scores: EngineScore[];
   19:   onProgress?: (progress: ReviewProgress) => void;
   20:   resolve: (scores: EngineScore[]) => void;
   21:   reject: (error: Error) => void;
   22: };
   23: 
   24: export class ReviewEngine {
   25:   private readonly worker: Worker;
   26:   private ready = false;
   27:   private disposed = false;
   28:   private latestScore: EngineScore | null = null;
   29:   private activeBatch: ReviewBatch | null = null;
   30:   private readyResolve!: () => void;
   31:   private readyReject!: (error: Error) => void;
   32: 
   33:   private readonly readyPromise = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
   34:     this.readyResolve = resolve;
   35:     this.readyReject = reject;
   36:   });
   37: 
   38:   constructor(workerUrl: string) {
   39:     // Mark readiness rejection as handled even when no analysis is awaiting it.
   40:     void this.readyPromise.catch(() => undefined);
   41: 
   42:     this.worker = new Worker(workerUrl);
   43: 
   44:     this.worker.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<string>) => {
   45:       this.handleLine(String(event.data));
   46:     };
   47: 
   48:     this.worker.onerror = (event) => {
   49:       this.fail(new Error(event.message || "Stockfish review worker failed."));
   50:     };
   51: 
   52:     this.worker.postMessage("uci");
   53:   }
   54: 
   55:   async analyzePositions(
   56:     fens: string[],
   57:     options: ReviewAnalysisOptions,
   58:   ): Promise<EngineScore[]> {
   59:     if (this.disposed) {
   60:       throw new Error("The review engine has been disposed.");
   61:     }
   62: 
   63:     if (this.activeBatch) {
   64:       throw new Error("A review analysis is already running.");
   65:     }
   66: 
   67:     if (fens.length === 0) {
   68:       return [];
   69:     }
   70: 
   71:     const depth = Math.max(1, Math.min(24, Math.floor(options.depth)));
   72: 
   73:     if (fens.some((fen) => !fen.trim())) {
   74:       throw new Error("Every review position must contain a valid FEN.");
   75:     }
   76: 
   77:     await this.readyPromise;
   78: 
   79:     if (this.disposed) {
   80:       throw new Error("The review engine has been disposed.");
   81:     }
   82: 
   83:     return new Promise<EngineScore[]>((resolve, reject) => {
   84:       this.activeBatch = {
   85:         fens: [...fens],
   86:         depth,
   87:         index: 0,
   88:         scores: [],
   89:         onProgress: options.onProgress,
   90:         resolve,
   91:         reject,
   92:       };
   93: 
   94:       this.startPosition();
   95:     });
   96:   }
   97: 
   98:   cancel(): void {
   99:     if (this.disposed) return;
  100: 
  101:     const batch = this.activeBatch;
  102:     this.activeBatch = null;
  103: 
  104:     this.disposed = true;
  105:     this.worker.terminate();
  106: 
  107:     const error = new Error("Review analysis was cancelled.");
  108:     batch?.reject(error);
  109:     this.readyReject(error);
  110:   }
  111: 
  112:   dispose(): void {
  113:     if (this.disposed) return;
  114: 
  115:     const batch = this.activeBatch;
  116:     this.activeBatch = null;
  117:     this.disposed = true;
  118: 
  119:     this.worker.terminate();
  120: 
  121:     const error = new Error("The review engine has been disposed.");
  122:     batch?.reject(error);
  123:     this.readyReject(error);
  124:   }
  125: 
  126:   private handleLine(line: string): void {
  127:     if (this.disposed) return;
  128: 
  129:     if (/^Error:/i.test(line)) {
  130:       this.fail(new Error(line));
  131:       return;
  132:     }
  133: 
  134:     if (line === "uciok") {
  135:       this.worker.postMessage("isready");
  136:       return;
  137:     }
  138: 
  139:     if (line === "readyok") {
  140:       this.ready = true;
  141:       this.readyResolve();
  142:       return;
  143:     }
  144: 
  145:     const batch = this.activeBatch;
  146:     if (!batch) return;
  147: 
  148:     if (line.startsWith("info ")) {
  149:       const sideToMove = batch.fens[batch.index].split(/\s+/)[1] === "b" ? "b" : "w";
  150:       const parsed = parseUciInfo(line, sideToMove);
  151: 
  152:       if (parsed && (!this.latestScore || parsed.depth >= this.latestScore.depth)) {
  153:         this.latestScore = parsed;
  154:       }
  155: 
  156:       return;
  157:     }
  158: 
  159:     if (!line.startsWith("bestmove ")) return;
  160: 
  161:     const bestMove = line.match(/^bestmove (\S+)/)?.[1];
  162: 
  163:     if (!this.latestScore) {
  164:       this.fail(new Error(`Stockfish returned no evaluation for ply ${batch.index + 1}.`));
  165:       return;
  166:     }
  167: 
  168:     const score: EngineScore = {
  169:       ...this.latestScore,
  170:       ...(bestMove && bestMove !== "(none)" ? { bestMove } : {}),
  171:     };
  172: 
  173:     batch.scores.push(score);
  174:     batch.index += 1;
  175: 
  176:     batch.onProgress?.({
  177:       completed: batch.index,
  178:       total: batch.fens.length,
  179:       currentPly: batch.index,
  180:     });
  181: 
  182:     this.latestScore = null;
  183: 
  184:     if (batch.index >= batch.fens.length) {
  185:       this.activeBatch = null;
  186:       batch.resolve(batch.scores);
  187:       return;
  188:     }
  189: 
  190:     this.startPosition();
  191:   }
  192: 
  193:   private startPosition(): void {
  194:     const batch = this.activeBatch;
  195:     if (!batch || this.disposed || !this.ready) return;
  196: 
  197:     this.latestScore = null;
  198: 
  199:     const fen = batch.fens[batch.index];
  200:     this.worker.postMessage(`position fen ${fen}`);
  201:     this.worker.postMessage(`go depth ${batch.depth}`);
  202:   }
  203: 
  204:   private fail(error: Error): void {
  205:     if (this.disposed) return;
  206: 
  207:     const batch = this.activeBatch;
  208:     this.activeBatch = null;
  209:     this.disposed = true;
  210: 
  211:     this.worker.terminate();
  212:     batch?.reject(error);
  213:     this.readyReject(error);
  214:   }
  215: }
  216:

FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\review-pgn.ts
==================================================
    1: import { Chess } from "chess.js";
    2: import { parsePgn } from "./pgn-import";
    3: 
    4: export type ReviewPosition = {
    5:   ply: number;
    6:   moveNumber: number;
    7:   color: "w" | "b";
    8:   san: string;
    9:   beforeFen: string;
   10:   afterFen: string;
   11:   from: string;
   12:   to: string;
   13:   promotion?: string;
   14:   flags: string;
   15: };
   16: 
   17: export type ReviewGame = {
   18:   headers: Record<string, string>;
   19:   startingFen: string;
   20:   finalFen: string;
   21:   positions: ReviewPosition[];
   22: };
   23: 
   24: export function reconstructReviewGame(pgn: string): ReviewGame {
   25:   const imported = parsePgn(pgn);
   26:   const game = new Chess(imported.lineBaseFen);
   27:   const positions: ReviewPosition[] = [];
   28: 
   29:   imported.moves.forEach((san, index) => {
   30:     const beforeFen = game.fen();
   31:     const move = game.move(san);
   32: 
   33:     positions.push({
   34:       ply: index + 1,
   35:       moveNumber: Math.floor(index / 2) + 1,
   36:       color: index % 2 === 0 ? "w" : "b",
   37:       san: move.san,
   38:       beforeFen,
   39:       afterFen: game.fen(),
   40:       from: move.from,
   41:       to: move.to,
   42:       ...(move.promotion ? { promotion: move.promotion } : {}),
   43:       flags: move.flags,
   44:     });
   45:   });
   46: 
   47:   if (game.fen() !== imported.finalFen) {
   48:     throw new Error("Review reconstruction does not match the imported PGN.");
   49:   }
   50: 
   51:   return {
   52:     headers: imported.headers,
   53:     startingFen: imported.lineBaseFen,
   54:     finalFen: game.fen(),
   55:     positions,
   56:   };
   57: }
   58: 
   59: export function getReviewFenAtPly(review: ReviewGame, ply: number): string {
   60:   const safePly = Math.max(0, Math.min(review.positions.length, Math.floor(ply)));
   61: 
   62:   return safePly === 0
   63:     ? review.startingFen
   64:     : review.positions[safePly - 1].afterFen;
   65: }

    1: import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

    4: class FakeWorker {
    5:   onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent<string>) => void) | null = null;
    6:   onerror: ((event: ErrorEvent) => void) | null = null;
    7:   commands: string[] = [];
    8:   terminated = false;
    9:   postMessage(command: string) { this.commands.push(command); }
   10:   terminate() { this.terminated = true; }
   11:   send(data: string) { this.onmessage?.({ data } as MessageEvent<string>); }
   12: }
   13: 
   14: afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals());

   16: describe("Stockfish UCI evaluation", () => {
   17:   it("converts side-to-move centipawns to the White perspective", () => {
   18:     expect(parseUciInfo("info depth 12 score cp 74 pv e2e4 e7e5", "w"))
   19:       .toEqual({ kind: "cp", value: 74, depth: 12, pv: ["e2e4", "e7e5"] });
   20:     expect(parseUciInfo("info depth 15 score cp 74 pv e7e5", "b"))
   21:       .toMatchObject({ kind: "cp", value: -74, depth: 15 });
   22:   });
   23: 
   24:   it("orients mate scores and represents a checkmated side-to-move", () => {

   26:     expect(parseUciInfo("info depth 20 score mate 3 pv e7e5", "b")?.value).toBe(-3);
   27:     expect(parseUciInfo("info depth 20 score mate 0", "w")?.value).toBe(-1);
   28:     expect(formatEvaluation({ kind: "mate", value: -3, depth: 20 })).toBe("#-3");
   29:   });
   30: 
   31:   it("keeps the evaluation bar bounded and consistent around equality", () => {

   33:     const blackAdvantage = { kind: "cp" as const, value: -250, depth: 12 };
   34:     expect(formatEvaluation(whiteAdvantage)).toBe("+2.50");
   35:     expect(whiteScoreShare(whiteAdvantage)).toBeGreaterThan(0.5);
   36:     expect(whiteScoreShare(blackAdvantage)).toBeLessThan(0.5);
   37:     expect(whiteScoreShare({ kind: "cp", value: 0, depth: 1 })).toBe(0.5);
   38:     expect(whiteScoreShare({ kind: "cp", value: 9999, depth: 1 })).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.96);
