==================================================
FILE: D:\chess\BookChess\ChessBookReader\src\stockfish.ts
==================================================
    1: export type EngineStatus = "off" | "loading" | "ready" | "analyzing" | "error";
    2: export type EngineScore = { kind: "cp" | "mate"; value: number; depth: number; bestMove?: string; pv?: string[] };
    3: 
    4: type UciRequest = { fen: string; depth: number };
    5: 
    6: export function parseUciInfo(line: string, sideToMove: "w" | "b"): Omit<EngineScore, "bestMove"> | null {
    7:   if (!line.startsWith("info ")) return null;
    8:   const depth = Number(line.match(/\bdepth (\d+)/)?.[1]);
    9:   const score = line.match(/\bscore (cp|mate) (-?\d+)/);
   10:   if (!score || !Number.isFinite(depth)) return null;
   11:   const value = Number(score[2]);
   12:   const whiteValue = sideToMove === "w" ? value : -value;
   13:   const kind = score[1] as "cp" | "mate";
   14:   const orientedValue = kind === "mate" && value === 0
   15:     ? (sideToMove === "w" ? -1 : 1)
   16:     : whiteValue;
   17:   const pv = line.match(/\bpv (.+)$/)?.[1]?.trim().split(/\s+/);
   18:   return {
   19:     kind,
   20:     value: orientedValue,
   21:     depth,
   22:     ...(pv?.length ? { pv } : {}),
   23:   };
   24: }
   25: 
   26: export function formatEvaluation(score: EngineScore): string {
   27:   if (score.kind === "mate") return `#${score.value > 0 ? "+" : ""}${score.value}`;
   28:   const pawns = score.value / 100;
   29:   return `${pawns > 0 ? "+" : ""}${pawns.toFixed(2)}`;
   30: }
   31: 
   32: export function whiteScoreShare(score: EngineScore): number {
   33:   if (score.kind === "mate") return score.value > 0 ? 0.96 : 0.04;
   34:   const bounded = Math.max(-1000, Math.min(1000, score.value));
   35:   return Math.max(0.04, Math.min(0.96, 1 / (1 + 10 ** (-bounded / 400))));
   36: }
   37: 
   38: export class StockfishEngine {
   39:   private readonly worker: Worker;
   40:   private ready = false;
   41:   private searching = false;
   42:   private awaitingStop = false;
   43:   private queued: UciRequest | null = null;
   44:   private currentSide: "w" | "b" = "w";
   45:   private latestScore: EngineScore | null = null;
   46: 
   47:   constructor(
   48:     workerUrl: string,
   49:     private readonly onStatus: (status: EngineStatus, message?: string) => void,
   50:     private readonly onScore: (score: EngineScore | null) => void,
   51:   ) {
   52:     this.worker = new Worker(workerUrl);
   53:     this.worker.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<string>) => this.handleLine(String(event.data));
   54:     this.worker.onerror = (event) => {
   55:       this.onStatus("error", event.message || "The Stockfish worker failed to load.");
   56:       this.dispose();
   57:     };
   58:     this.onStatus("loading");
   59:     this.worker.postMessage("uci");
   60:   }
   61: 
   62:   analyze(fen: string, depth: number): void {
   63:     this.queued = { fen, depth: Math.max(1, Math.min(24, Math.floor(depth))) };
   64:     this.latestScore = null;
   65:     this.onScore(null);
   66:     if (!this.ready) return;
   67:     if (this.searching) {
   68:       if (!this.awaitingStop) {
   69:         this.awaitingStop = true;
   70:         this.worker.postMessage("stop");
   71:       }
   72:       return;
   73:     }
   74:     this.startQueuedAnalysis();
   75:   }
   76: 
   77:   dispose(): void {
   78:     this.worker.terminate();
   79:     this.ready = false;
   80:     this.searching = false;
   81:     this.queued = null;
   82:   }
   83: 
   84:   private handleLine(line: string): void {
   85:     if (/^Error:/i.test(line)) {
   86:       this.onStatus("error", line);
   87:       this.dispose();
   88:       return;
   89:     }
   90:     if (line === "uciok") {
   91:       this.worker.postMessage("isready");
   92:       return;
   93:     }
   94:     if (line === "readyok") {
   95:       this.ready = true;
   96:       this.onStatus("ready");
   97:       this.startQueuedAnalysis();
   98:       return;
   99:     }
  100:     if (line.startsWith("info ")) {
  101:       if (this.awaitingStop) return;
  102:       const score = parseUciInfo(line, this.currentSide);
  103:       if (score) {
  104:         // Use the current principal variation's first move for a live arrow.
  105:         // Stockfish's final "bestmove" will replace this provisional move.
  106:         const provisionalMove = score.pv?.[0];
  107:         this.latestScore = {
  108:           ...score,
  109:           ...(provisionalMove ? { bestMove: provisionalMove } : {}),
  110:         };
  111:         this.onScore(this.latestScore);
  112:       }
  113:       return;
  114:     }
  115:     const bestMove = line.match(/^bestmove (\S+)/)?.[1];
  116:     if (!bestMove) return;
  117:     this.searching = false;
  118:     if (this.awaitingStop) {
  119:       this.awaitingStop = false;
  120:       this.startQueuedAnalysis();
  121:       return;
  122:     }
  123:     if (this.latestScore) {
  124:       this.latestScore = { ...this.latestScore, bestMove: bestMove === "(none)" ? undefined : bestMove };
  125:       this.onScore(this.latestScore);
  126:     }
  127:     this.onStatus("ready");
  128:   }
  129: 
  130:   private startQueuedAnalysis(): void {
  131:     if (!this.ready || this.searching || !this.queued) return;
  132:     const request = this.queued;
  133:     this.queued = null;
  134:     this.currentSide = request.fen.split(/\s+/)[1] === "b" ? "b" : "w";
  135:     this.searching = true;
  136:     this.latestScore = null;
  137:     this.onStatus("analyzing");
  138:     this.worker.postMessage(`position fen ${request.fen}`);
  139:     this.worker.postMessage(`go depth ${request.depth}`);
  140:   }
  141: }

    1: import "fake-indexeddb/auto";
    2: import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
    3: import { newSession, START_FEN } from "./model";
    4: import { getActiveBookId, getSession, putSession, setActiveBookId } from "./storage";
    5: 
    6: afterEach(async () => {
    7:   await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    8:     const request = indexedDB.deleteDatabase("chessbook-reader");
    9:     request.onsuccess = () => resolve();
   10:     request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
   11:   });
   12: });
   13: 
   14: describe("per-book IndexedDB reading sessions", () => {
   15:   it("restores exact analysis state independently for each book", async () => {
   16:     const first = newSession("book-a");
   17:     first.page = 42;
   18:     first.selectedDiagramId = "diagram-3";
   19:     first.originalFen = START_FEN;
   20:     first.lineBaseFen = START_FEN;
   21:     first.moves = ["e4", "e5", "Nf3"];
   22:     first.moveIndex = 3;
   23:     first.currentFen = "rnbqkbnr/pppp1ppp/8/4p3/4P3/5N2/PPPP1PPP/RNBQKB1R b KQkq - 1 2";
   24:     first.flipped = true;
   25: 
   26:     const second = newSession("book-b");
   27:     second.page = 118;
   28:     second.currentFen = "rnbqkbnr/pppppppp/8/8/8/8/PPPPPPPP/RNBQKBNR w KQkq - 0 1";
   29: 
   30:     await Promise.all([putSession(first), putSession(second), setActiveBookId("book-a")]);
   31:     const restoredFirst = await getSession("book-a");
   32:     const restoredSecond = await getSession("book-b");
   33: 
   34:     expect(await getActiveBookId()).toBe("book-a");
   35:     expect(restoredFirst).toMatchObject({
   36:       page: 42,
   37:       selectedDiagramId: "diagram-3",
   38:       currentFen: first.currentFen,
   39:       moves: ["e4", "e5", "Nf3"],
   40:       moveIndex: 3,
   41:       flipped: true,
   42:     });
   43:     expect(restoredSecond).toMatchObject({ page: 118, currentFen: second.currentFen, moves: [] });
   44:   });
   45: });
